import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import request from 'supertest';
import type { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';
import { TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_RESOLVER, type TemplateManagementResolver } from '../src/domain/message-templates/template-management.ports';
import { TemplateDraftService } from '../src/domain/message-templates/template-draft.service';
import { TemplateSubmissionService } from '../src/domain/message-templates/template-submission.service';
import { TEMPLATE_TRANSACTION_BOUNDARY, type TemplateTransactionBoundary } from '../src/domain/message-templates/template-transaction-boundary';
import { TEMPLATE_OBSERVATION_CLOCK, TemplateObservationService, type TemplateObservationClock } from '../src/domain/message-templates/template-observation.service';
import { sealChannelCredential } from '../src/domain/channels/channel-credential-crypto';
import { templateDefinitionSemanticFingerprint } from '../src/domain/message-templates/template-definition';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { criarCanalVerificado, criarMantenedora, criarUsuario, limparBanco, login, META_APP_SECRET, subirApp } from './helpers';

function response(status: number, payload: unknown): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(payload), { status, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
}

function deferred<T>() {
  let resolve!: (value: T) => void;
  const promise = new Promise<T>((done) => { resolve = done; });
  return { promise, resolve };
}

async function withReadbackFailure<T>(service: TemplateSubmissionService,
  model: 'messageTemplateOperation' | 'messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt', action: 'findFirst', run: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  const target = service as unknown as { prisma: PrismaService };
  const original = target.prisma;
  let pending = true;
  target.prisma = new Proxy(original, { get(prisma, property) {
    const delegate = Reflect.get(prisma, property, prisma);
    if (property !== model || !delegate || typeof delegate !== 'object') return delegate;
    return new Proxy(delegate as object, { get(modelDelegate, method) {
      const value = Reflect.get(modelDelegate, method, modelDelegate);
      if (method === action && pending) {
        pending = false;
        return () => Promise.reject(new Error('injected PostgreSQL readback unavailable'));
      }
      return value;
    } });
  } });
  try { return await run(); }
  finally { target.prisma = original; }
}

describe('scoped template-management provider (PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof subirApp>>['app'];
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let buId: string;
  let buId2: string;
  let channel1: string;
  let channel2: string;
  let ownerId: string;
  let ownerEmail: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    const originalDatabaseUrl = process.env.DATABASE_URL;
    if (!originalDatabaseUrl) throw new Error('A configuração descartável de PostgreSQL não está disponível.');
    const boundedDatabaseUrl = new URL(originalDatabaseUrl);
    boundedDatabaseUrl.searchParams.set('connection_limit', '5');
    process.env.DATABASE_URL = boundedDatabaseUrl.toString();
    try { ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp(undefined, true)); }
    finally { process.env.DATABASE_URL = originalDatabaseUrl; }
  });
  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    ownerEmail = `template-provider-${Date.now()}@example.test`;
    const tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Template provider tenant', ownerEmail);
    buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    ownerId = tenant.owner.id;
    const first = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, buId, 'OFICIAL', `template-phone-a-${Date.now()}`);
    const firstBu = await prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: buId }, select: { setorId: true } });
    const secondBu = await prisma.businessUnit.create({ data: {
      mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id, setorId: firstBu.setorId, name: `Template second number ${Date.now()}`,
    } });
    buId2 = secondBu.id;
    await prisma.userBuRole.create({ data: { userId: ownerId, buId: buId2, role: 'OWNER', grantedViaTipo: 'DIRETO' } });
    const second = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, buId2, 'OFICIAL', `template-phone-b-${Date.now()}`);
    channel1 = first.channel.id;
    channel2 = second.channel.id;
    await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: channel1 }, data: { wabaId: 'waba-a', status: 'DESCONECTADO', receiptVerifiedAt: null } });
    await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: channel2 }, data: { wabaId: 'waba-b', status: 'DESCONECTADO', receiptVerifiedAt: null } });
    const keyId = process.env.CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY_ID!;
    const key = Buffer.from(process.env.CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY!, 'base64');
    const otherAccountCredential = sealChannelCredential('fixture-account-b-token', keyId, key, {
      purpose: 'active', provider: 'OFICIAL', buId: buId2, channelId: channel2,
      generation: second.channel.generation, version: 1,
    });
    await prisma.channelCredential.update({ where: { channelId: channel2 }, data: {
      keyId, nonce: new Uint8Array(otherAccountCredential.nonce),
      ciphertext: new Uint8Array(otherAccountCredential.ciphertext), tag: new Uint8Array(otherAccountCredential.tag),
    } });
  });
  afterAll(async () => { if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma); await app?.close(); });

  it('resolves capability without network or decryption and uses one scoped grant across pages and create', async () => {
    const fetchMock = vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } }))
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(response(200, { data: [
        { id: 'remote-1', name: 'welcome_user', language: 'pt_BR', status: 'APPROVED', category: 'UTILITY', parameter_format: 'NAMED', components: [{ type: 'BODY', text: 'Olá {{first_name}}.' }] },
      ], paging: { next: 'https://graph.facebook.com/v26.0/opaque', cursors: { after: 'cursor-one' } } }))
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(response(200, { data: [
        { id: 'remote-2', name: 'other_user', language: 'pt_BR', status: 'PENDING', category: 'UTILITY', parameter_format: 'POSITIONAL', components: [{ type: 'BODY', text: 'Oi {{1}}.' }] },
      ] }))
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(response(200, { id: 'remote-created' }));
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      const resolver = app.get(TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_RESOLVER) as TemplateManagementResolver;
      const resolved = await resolver.resolve({ buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1 });
      expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(resolved.context).not.toHaveProperty('accessToken');
      expect(resolved.context).not.toHaveProperty('wabaId');
      expect(resolved.context.accountReference).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);

      const page = await resolved.provider.listTemplates({ context: resolved.context, after: null, limit: 50 });
      expect(page.templates.map((template) => template.remoteTemplateId)).toEqual(['remote-1']);
      expect(page.templates[0]).toMatchObject({ category: 'UTILITY', parameterFormat: 'NAMED', status: 'APPROVED' });
      expect(page.nextCursor).toBe('cursor-one');
      const page2 = await resolved.provider.listTemplates({ context: resolved.context, after: page.nextCursor, limit: 50 });
      expect(page2.templates.map((template) => template.remoteTemplateId)).toEqual(['remote-2']);
      expect(page2.nextCursor).toBeNull();
      const created = await resolved.provider.createTemplate({ context: resolved.context, definition: {
        name: 'hello_user', language: 'pt_BR', category: 'UTILITY', body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' },
      } });
      expect(created).toEqual({ kind: 'ACCEPTED', dispatched: true, remoteTemplateId: 'remote-created' });
      expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(4);
      expect(fetchMock.mock.calls.filter(([url]) => String(url).includes('/debug_token'))).toHaveLength(1);
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('does not share a grant between independently bound accounts', async () => {
    const calls: Array<{ url: string; init: RequestInit }> = [];
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input);
      calls.push({ url, init });
      const parsed = new URL(url);
      if (parsed.pathname.endsWith('/debug_token')) {
        const inputToken = parsed.searchParams.get('input_token');
        const target = inputToken === 'fixture-scoped-token' ? 'waba-a'
          : inputToken === 'fixture-account-b-token' ? 'waba-b' : 'unexpected';
        return response(200, { data: {
          app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
          granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: [target] }],
        } });
      }
      if (parsed.pathname.endsWith('/message_templates')) return response(200, { data: [] });
      return response(404, { error: { message: 'unknown fixture request' } });
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      const resolver = app.get(TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_RESOLVER) as TemplateManagementResolver;
      const first = await resolver.resolve({ buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1 });
      const second = await resolver.resolve({ buId: buId2, channelId: channel2, expectedGeneration: 1 });
      await expect(first.provider.listTemplates({ context: second.context, after: null, limit: 20 }))
        .rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_AUTHORIZATION_UNAVAILABLE' });
      expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      await Promise.all([
        first.provider.listTemplates({ context: first.context, after: null, limit: 20 }),
        second.provider.listTemplates({ context: second.context, after: null, limit: 20 }),
      ]);
      expect(calls).toHaveLength(4);
      const debugCalls = calls.filter(({ url }) => new URL(url).pathname.endsWith('/debug_token'));
      expect(debugCalls.map(({ url }) => new URL(url).searchParams.get('input_token')).sort()).toEqual([
        'fixture-account-b-token', 'fixture-scoped-token',
      ]);
      expect(debugCalls.every(({ init }) => (init.headers as Record<string, string>).authorization === `Bearer fixture-app|${META_APP_SECRET}`)).toBe(true);
      const graphCalls = calls.filter(({ url }) => new URL(url).pathname.endsWith('/message_templates'));
      expect(graphCalls.map(({ url, init }) => ({ url, method: init.method, authorization: (init.headers as Record<string, string>).authorization })).sort((a, b) => a.url.localeCompare(b.url))).toEqual([
        { url: 'https://graph.facebook.com/v26.0/waba-a/message_templates?fields=id%2Cname%2Clanguage%2Cstatus%2Ccategory%2Cparameter_format%2Ccomponents&limit=20', method: 'GET', authorization: 'Bearer fixture-scoped-token' },
        { url: 'https://graph.facebook.com/v26.0/waba-b/message_templates?fields=id%2Cname%2Clanguage%2Cstatus%2Ccategory%2Cparameter_format%2Ccomponents&limit=20', method: 'GET', authorization: 'Bearer fixture-account-b-token' },
      ]);
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('defers credential decryption until the provider is called', async () => {
    await prisma.channelCredential.update({ where: { channelId: channel1 }, data: { ciphertext: new Uint8Array([1, 2, 3]) } });
    const fetchMock = vi.fn();
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      const resolver = app.get(TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_RESOLVER) as TemplateManagementResolver;
      const resolved = await resolver.resolve({ buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1 });
      expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      await expect(resolved.provider.listTemplates({ context: resolved.context, after: null, limit: 10 })).rejects.toBeDefined();
      expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('fails closed when channel generation rotates while the grant check is held before dispatch', async () => {
    const draft = await app.get(TemplateDraftService).createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1,
      expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: 'held-grant-generation-rotation',
      definition: { name: 'held_grant_rotation', language: 'pt_BR', category: 'UTILITY',
        body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } } });
    const grantStarted = deferred<void>();
    const grantHold = deferred<Response>();
    let pending: ReturnType<TemplateSubmissionService['submitDraft']> | undefined;
    let listGets = 0;
    let posts = 0;
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input); const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') {
        grantStarted.resolve();
        return grantHold.promise;
      }
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') { listGets += 1; return response(200, { data: [] }); }
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') { posts += 1; return response(200, { id: 'must-not-dispatch' }); }
      throw new Error(`unexpected held-grant request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      pending = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService).submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: draft.id,
        expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint,
        idempotencyKey: 'ac120b9a-50d0-4e33-bc2d-dc5899d540a1' });
      await grantStarted.promise;
      await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: channel1 }, data: { generation: 2 } });
      grantHold.resolve(response(200, { data: { app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true,
        scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'], granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }] } }));
      const result = await pending;
      expect(result).toMatchObject({ phase: 'REJECTED', safeCode: 'PRE_DISPATCH_UNAVAILABLE' });
      expect(listGets).toBe(1);
      expect(posts).toBe(0);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.count({ where: { operationId: result.id } })).toBe(0);
    } finally {
      grantHold.resolve(response(200, { data: { app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true,
        scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'], granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }] } }));
      if (pending) await Promise.allSettled([pending]);
      vi.unstubAllGlobals();
    }
  });

  it('accepts same-account reauthorization on the historical channel and returns a current fingerprint', async () => {
    const service = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const definition = { name: 'hello_user', language: 'pt_BR' as const, category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const draft = await service.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: 'same-account-reauth', definition });
    const oldFingerprint = draft.definitionFingerprint;
    const keyId = process.env.CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY_ID!;
    const key = Buffer.from(process.env.CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY!, 'base64');
    const renewed = sealChannelCredential('fixture-renewed-token', keyId, key, {
      purpose: 'active', provider: 'OFICIAL', buId, channelId: channel1, generation: 2, version: 2,
    });
    await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: channel1 }, data: { generation: 2 } });
    await prisma.channelCredential.update({ where: { channelId: channel1 }, data: {
      generation: 2, version: 2, keyId, nonce: new Uint8Array(renewed.nonce),
      ciphertext: new Uint8Array(renewed.ciphertext), tag: new Uint8Array(renewed.tag),
    } });
    const saved = await service.saveDraft(ownerId, {
      buId, draftId: draft.id, expectedGeneration: 2, expectedRevision: 1,
      definition: { ...definition, body: 'Atualização {{first_name}}.' },
    });
    expect(saved.channelId).toBe(channel1);
    expect(saved.revision).toBe(2);
    expect(saved.definitionFingerprint).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);
    expect(saved.definitionFingerprint).not.toBe(oldFingerprint);
  });

  it('fences twenty concurrent saves through each transaction connection without pool starvation', async () => {
    const service = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const createdDrafts = await Promise.allSettled(Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, index) => service.createDraft(ownerId, {
      buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: `pool-fence-${index}`,
      definition: { name: `pool_fence_${index}`, language: 'pt_BR', category: 'UTILITY', body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } },
    })));
    expect(createdDrafts.every((result) => result.status === 'fulfilled')).toBe(true);
    const drafts = createdDrafts.map((result) => {
      if (result.status === 'rejected') throw result.reason;
      return result.value;
    });
    const saveResults = await Promise.allSettled(drafts.map((draft, index) => service.saveDraft(ownerId, {
      buId, draftId: draft.id, expectedGeneration: 1, expectedRevision: 1,
      definition: { name: `pool_fence_${index}`, language: 'pt_BR', category: 'UTILITY', body: `Oi {{first_name}} ${index}.`, examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } },
    })));
    expect(saveResults.every((result) => result.status === 'fulfilled')).toBe(true);
    const saves = saveResults.map((result) => {
      if (result.status === 'rejected') throw result.reason;
      return result.value;
    });
    expect(saves).toHaveLength(20);
    expect(saves.every((saved) => saved.revision === 2)).toBe(true);
    expect(await prisma.messageTemplateRevision.count({ where: { draftId: { in: drafts.map((draft) => draft.id) } } })).toBe(40);
  }, 20_000);

  it('blocks a changed WABA with CONTA_ALTERADA and keeps the historical binding', async () => {
    const service = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const definition = { name: 'hello_user', language: 'pt_BR' as const, category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const draft = await service.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: 'account-change', definition });
    await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: channel1 }, data: { wabaId: 'waba-replaced' } });
    await expect(service.saveDraft(ownerId, {
      buId, draftId: draft.id, expectedGeneration: 1, expectedRevision: 1,
      definition: { ...definition, body: 'Não vincular {{first_name}}.' },
    })).rejects.toMatchObject({ response: { code: 'CONTA_ALTERADA' } });
    expect(await prisma.messageTemplateRevision.count({ where: { draftId: draft.id } })).toBe(1);
    expect((await service.getDraft(ownerId, buId, draft.id)).channelId).toBe(channel1);
  });

  it('submits one durable operation and replays the same receipt without another provider call', async () => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const definition = { name: 'durable_submit', language: 'pt_BR' as const, category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const draft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: 'durable-submit', definition });
    const calls: Array<{ url: string; method: string; body?: string }> = [];
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input);
      const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      calls.push({ url, method, ...(typeof init.body === 'string' ? { body: init.body } : {}) });
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: [] });
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') return response(200, { id: 'durable-remote-id' });
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      const input = { draftId: draft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: '9a1544fb-7b3a-4cdb-a170-3fef0d67d6cd' };
      const accepted = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, input);
      expect(accepted).toMatchObject({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', remoteTemplateId: 'durable-remote-id' });
      const callsAfterAccept = fetchMock.mock.calls.length;
      const replay = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, input);
      expect(replay).toMatchObject({ id: accepted.id, phase: 'ACCEPTED', remoteTemplateId: 'durable-remote-id' });
      expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(callsAfterAccept);
      expect(calls.filter(({ url }) => url.includes('/debug_token'))).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(calls.filter(({ url, method }) => url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET')).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(calls.filter(({ url, method }) => url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST')).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(calls.find(({ method }) => method === 'POST')?.body).toBe(JSON.stringify({ name: 'durable_submit', language: 'pt_BR', category: 'UTILITY', parameter_format: 'NAMED', components: [
        { type: 'BODY', text: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', example: { body_text_named_params: [{ param_name: 'first_name', example: 'Ana' }] } },
      ] }));
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.count({ where: { id: accepted.id, phase: 'ACCEPTED' } })).toBe(1);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.count({ where: { operationId: accepted.id } })).toBe(3);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.count({ where: { operationId: accepted.id } })).toBe(1);
      expect((await prisma.messageTemplateDraft.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: draft.id } })).state).toBe('LOCKED');
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('rejects a contradictory HTTP 200 preflight before dispatch and releases the draft reservation', async () => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const definition = { name: 'preflight_contradiction', language: 'pt_BR' as const,
      category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const draft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1,
      expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: 'preflight-contradiction', definition });
    const calls: Array<{ url: string; method: string }> = [];
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input); const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      calls.push({ url, method });
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') {
        return response(200, { data: [], error: { type: 'OAuthException', code: 190, message: 'contradictory preflight' } });
      }
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      const cookie = await login(app, ownerEmail);
      const submitted = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/message-templates/${draft.id}/submit`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
        .set('idempotency-key', '791ff384-74ba-4ff7-b356-a5a2fd5fd2c4')
        .send({ expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
          expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint });
      expect(submitted.status).toBe(503);
      expect(submitted.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'TEMPLATE_PREFLIGHT_UNAVAILABLE', phase: 'REJECTED', receiptPersistence: 'RECORDED' });
      expect(calls.filter(({ url, method }) => url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST')).toHaveLength(0);
      expect(calls.filter(({ url, method }) => url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET')).toHaveLength(1);
      const operation = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: draft.id } });
      expect(operation).toMatchObject({ phase: 'REJECTED', safeCode: 'TEMPLATE_PREFLIGHT_UNAVAILABLE', dispatchClaimId: null });
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.findMany({ where: { operationId: operation.id },
        orderBy: { sequence: 'asc' }, select: { sequence: true, phase: true, safeCode: true } })).toEqual([
        { sequence: 1, phase: 'PREPARED', safeCode: 'RESERVED' },
        { sequence: 2, phase: 'REJECTED', safeCode: 'TEMPLATE_PREFLIGHT_UNAVAILABLE' },
      ]);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.count({ where: { operationId: operation.id } })).toBe(0);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateDraft.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: draft.id }, select: { state: true, revision: true } }))
        .toEqual({ state: 'DRAFT', revision: 1 });
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('projects a saved individual observation through draft and operation reads without provider calls or writes', async () => {
    const definition = { name: 'saved_paused_observation', language: 'pt_BR' as const,
      category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const draft = await app.get(TemplateDraftService).createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1,
      expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: 'saved-paused-observation', definition });
    const remoteId = 'saved-paused-remote';
    const paused = { id: remoteId, name: definition.name, language: definition.language, status: 'PAUSED',
      category: definition.category, parameter_format: 'NAMED', components: [{ type: 'BODY', text: definition.body }] };
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input); const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: [] });
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') return response(200, { id: remoteId });
      if (url.includes(`/${remoteId}?`) && method === 'GET') return response(200, paused);
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    const clock = app.get<TemplateObservationClock>(TEMPLATE_OBSERVATION_CLOCK);
    let now = new Date();
    const clockSpy = vi.spyOn(clock, 'now').mockImplementation(() => new Date(now));
    try {
      const cookie = await login(app, ownerEmail);
      const submitted = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/message-templates/${draft.id}/submit`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
        .set('idempotency-key', 'a38f19d7-c9f5-4e25-8c2b-9f2a3a7e042f')
        .send({ expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint });
      expect(submitted.status).toBe(201);
      const operationId = submitted.body.id as string;
      const catalogPageCount = await prisma.messageTemplateCatalogPage.count();
      expect(catalogPageCount).toBe(0);
      const refresh = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/message-templates/${draft.id}/refresh`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({});
      expect(refresh.status).toBe(200);
      expect(refresh.body).toMatchObject({ status: 'PAUSED', available: true, eligible: false, eligibleForUse: false });
      const callsAfterRefresh = fetchMock.mock.calls.length;
      const observedIdentity = await prisma.messageTemplateRemoteObservation.findFirstOrThrow({
        where: { remoteTemplateId: remoteId }, select: { contextKey: true },
      });
      const contextKey = observedIdentity.contextKey;
      const storedBefore = await prisma.messageTemplateRemoteObservation.findUniqueOrThrow({
        where: { contextKey_remoteTemplateId: { contextKey, remoteTemplateId: remoteId } },
      });
      const opBefore = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: operationId } });
      const receiptCountBefore = await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.count({ where: { operationId } });

      const detail = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/message-templates/${draft.id}`).set('Cookie', cookie);
      const operation = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/message-templates/${draft.id}/operations/${operationId}`).set('Cookie', cookie);
      expect(detail.status).toBe(200);
      expect(operation.status).toBe(200);
      expect(detail.body.savedObservation).toMatchObject({ status: 'PAUSED', available: true,
        eligible: false, eligibleForUse: false, matchesDefinition: true });
      expect(detail.body.currentOperation.savedObservation).toMatchObject({ status: 'PAUSED', observedFence: refresh.body.observedFence });
      expect(operation.body.savedObservation).toMatchObject({ status: 'PAUSED', observedFence: refresh.body.observedFence });
      expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(callsAfterRefresh);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateCatalogPage.count()).toBe(0);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateRemoteObservation.findUniqueOrThrow({
        where: { contextKey_remoteTemplateId: { contextKey, remoteTemplateId: remoteId } },
      })).toEqual(storedBefore);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: operationId } })).toEqual(opBefore);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.count({ where: { operationId } })).toBe(receiptCountBefore);

      now = new Date(now.getTime() + 301_000);
      const expired = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/message-templates/${draft.id}`).set('Cookie', cookie);
      expect(expired.body.savedObservation).toMatchObject({ status: 'PAUSED', available: false, eligible: false, eligibleForUse: false });
      expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(callsAfterRefresh);
      await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: channel1 }, data: { wabaId: 'waba-saved-observation-replaced' } });
      const staleContext = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/message-templates/${draft.id}`).set('Cookie', cookie);
      expect(staleContext.status).toBe(200);
      expect(staleContext.body.savedObservation).toMatchObject({ status: 'PAUSED', available: false,
        eligible: false, eligibleForUse: false, safeCode: 'TEMPLATE_CONTEXT_STALE' });
      expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(callsAfterRefresh);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateRemoteObservation.findUniqueOrThrow({
        where: { contextKey_remoteTemplateId: { contextKey, remoteTemplateId: remoteId } },
      })).toEqual(storedBefore);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateCatalogPage.count()).toBe(0);
    } finally { clockSpy.mockRestore(); vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('projects a refreshed observation from the current same-account generation without rebinding the operation', async () => {
    const definition = { name: 'same_account_observation_rotation', language: 'pt_BR' as const,
      category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const draft = await app.get(TemplateDraftService).createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1,
      expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: 'same-account-observation-rotation', definition });
    const remoteId = 'same-account-observation-remote';
    const paused = { id: remoteId, name: definition.name, language: definition.language, status: 'PAUSED',
      category: definition.category, parameter_format: 'NAMED', components: [{ type: 'BODY', text: definition.body }] };
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input); const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: [] });
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') return response(200, { id: remoteId });
      if (url.includes(`/${remoteId}?`) && method === 'GET') return response(200, paused);
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      const cookie = await login(app, ownerEmail);
      const submitted = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/message-templates/${draft.id}/submit`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
        .set('idempotency-key', '67b42d03-9a47-42e4-9b0c-dfe7fb5183ec')
        .send({ expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint });
      expect(submitted.status).toBe(201);
      const operationId = submitted.body.id as string;
      const operationBefore = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: operationId },
        select: { id: true, channelId: true, channelGeneration: true, activeCredentialVersion: true, accountReference: true,
          requestHash: true, dispatchClaimId: true, remoteTemplateId: true, acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId: true } });
      expect(operationBefore).toMatchObject({ channelId: channel1, channelGeneration: 1, activeCredentialVersion: 1,
        remoteTemplateId: remoteId, acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId: remoteId });

      const keyId = process.env.CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY_ID!;
      const key = Buffer.from(process.env.CHANNEL_CREDENTIAL_KEY!, 'base64');
      const renewed = sealChannelCredential('fixture-same-account-renewed-token', keyId, key, {
        purpose: 'active', provider: 'OFICIAL', buId, channelId: channel1, generation: 2, version: 2,
      });
      await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: channel1 }, data: { generation: 2 } });
      await prisma.channelCredential.update({ where: { channelId: channel1 }, data: {
        generation: 2, version: 2, keyId, nonce: new Uint8Array(renewed.nonce),
        ciphertext: new Uint8Array(renewed.ciphertext), tag: new Uint8Array(renewed.tag),
      } });

      const refresh = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/message-templates/${draft.id}/refresh`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({});
      expect(refresh.status).toBe(200);
      expect(refresh.body).toMatchObject({ remoteTemplateId: remoteId, status: 'PAUSED', available: true,
        eligible: false, eligibleForUse: false });
      expect(refresh.body.observedAt).toEqual(expect.any(String));
      expect(refresh.body.observedFence).toMatch(/^\d+$/);
      const callsAfterExplicitRefresh = fetchMock.mock.calls.length;
      const observationBeforeReads = await prisma.messageTemplateRemoteObservation.findFirstOrThrow({
        where: { remoteTemplateId: remoteId },
      });
      const receiptsBeforeReads = await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.findMany({ where: { operationId },
        orderBy: { sequence: 'asc' } });

      const detail = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/message-templates/${draft.id}`).set('Cookie', cookie);
      const operation = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/message-templates/${draft.id}/operations/${operationId}`).set('Cookie', cookie);
      expect(detail.status).toBe(200);
      expect(operation.status).toBe(200);
      expect(detail.body.savedObservation).toMatchObject({ remoteTemplateId: remoteId, status: 'PAUSED', available: true,
        observedAt: refresh.body.observedAt, observedFence: refresh.body.observedFence });
      expect(detail.body.currentOperation.savedObservation).toMatchObject({ status: 'PAUSED',
        observedAt: refresh.body.observedAt, observedFence: refresh.body.observedFence });
      expect(operation.body.savedObservation).toMatchObject({ status: 'PAUSED',
        observedAt: refresh.body.observedAt, observedFence: refresh.body.observedFence });
      expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(callsAfterExplicitRefresh);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateRemoteObservation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { remoteTemplateId: remoteId } }))
        .toEqual(observationBeforeReads);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.findMany({ where: { operationId }, orderBy: { sequence: 'asc' } }))
        .toEqual(receiptsBeforeReads);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: operationId },
        select: { id: true, channelId: true, channelGeneration: true, activeCredentialVersion: true, accountReference: true,
          requestHash: true, dispatchClaimId: true, remoteTemplateId: true, acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId: true } })).toEqual(operationBefore);

      await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: channel1 }, data: { wabaId: 'waba-same-account-test-rebound' } });
      const changedAccountDetail = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/message-templates/${draft.id}`).set('Cookie', cookie);
      const changedAccountOperation = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/message-templates/${draft.id}/operations/${operationId}`).set('Cookie', cookie);
      expect(changedAccountDetail.status).toBe(200);
      expect(changedAccountOperation.status).toBe(200);
      for (const historical of [changedAccountDetail.body.savedObservation, changedAccountOperation.body.savedObservation]) {
        if (historical === null) continue;
        expect(historical).toMatchObject({ status: 'PAUSED', available: false,
          eligible: false, eligibleForUse: false, safeCode: 'TEMPLATE_CONTEXT_STALE' });
      }
      expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(callsAfterExplicitRefresh);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateRemoteObservation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { remoteTemplateId: remoteId } }))
        .toEqual(observationBeforeReads);
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('persists a held original ACK after account context changes but keeps its observation ineligible', async () => {
    const definition = { name: 'context_changes_during_ack', language: 'pt_BR' as const,
      category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const draft = await app.get(TemplateDraftService).createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1,
      expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: 'context-changes-during-ack', definition });
    const resolved = await app.get<TemplateManagementResolver>(TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_RESOLVER)
      .resolve({ buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1 });
    const binding = { mantenedoraId: resolved.context.mantenedoraId, buId: resolved.context.buId,
      channelId: resolved.context.channelId, accountReference: resolved.context.accountReference,
      generation: resolved.context.generation, pairingEpoch: resolved.context.pairingEpoch,
      providerResourceIdentityId: resolved.context.providerResourceIdentityId,
      activeCredentialVersion: resolved.context.activeCredentialVersion };
    const contextKey = createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(binding).sort(([left], [right]) => left.localeCompare(right)),
    ))).digest('hex');
    const remoteId = 'held-context-ack-remote';
    const now = new Date();
    await prisma.messageTemplateObservationLease.create({ data: { contextKey,
      mantenedoraId: resolved.context.mantenedoraId, buId, channelId: channel1,
      accountReference: resolved.context.accountReference, channelGeneration: resolved.context.generation,
      pairingEpoch: resolved.context.pairingEpoch, providerResourceIdentityId: resolved.context.providerResourceIdentityId,
      activeCredentialVersion: resolved.context.activeCredentialVersion ?? 0, fence: 1n } });
    await prisma.messageTemplateRemoteObservation.create({ data: { contextKey, remoteTemplateId: remoteId,
      name: definition.name, language: definition.language, status: 'APPROVED', category: definition.category,
      parameterFormat: 'NAMED', components: [{ type: 'BODY', text: definition.body }],
      semanticFingerprint: templateDefinitionSemanticFingerprint(definition), supported: true,
      available: true, observedEligible: true, observedAt: now, observedFence: 1n } });

    const postStarted = deferred<void>();
    const postHold = deferred<Response>();
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input); const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: [] });
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') {
        postStarted.resolve();
        return postHold.promise;
      }
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    let submitPromise: Promise<{ status: number; body: Record<string, unknown> }> | undefined;
    try {
      const cookie = await login(app, ownerEmail);
      submitPromise = request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/message-templates/${draft.id}/submit`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', randomUUID())
        .send({ expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint })
        .then((result) => ({ status: result.status, body: result.body as Record<string, unknown> }));
      await postStarted.promise;
      await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: channel1 }, data: { wabaId: 'waba-after-dispatch' } });
      postHold.resolve(response(200, { id: remoteId }));
      const submitted = await submitPromise;
      expect(submitted.status).toBe(201);
      expect(submitted.body).toMatchObject({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', providerAcceptance: 'CONFIRMED',
        acceptanceProvenance: 'ACKNOWLEDGED', remoteTemplateId: remoteId,
        savedObservation: { status: 'APPROVED', available: false, eligible: false,
          eligibleForUse: false, safeCode: 'TEMPLATE_CONTEXT_STALE' } });
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: draft.id } }))
        .toMatchObject({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId: remoteId });
      expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
    } finally {
      postHold.resolve(response(200, { id: remoteId }));
      if (submitPromise) await submitPromise.catch(() => undefined);
      vi.unstubAllGlobals();
    }
  });

  it('keeps a post-dispatch timeout ambiguous and same-key replay performs no second POST', async () => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const definition = { name: 'uncertain_submit', language: 'pt_BR' as const, category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const draft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: 'uncertain-submit', definition });
    const calls: Array<{ url: string; method: string }> = [];
    let listCalls = 0;
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input);
      const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      calls.push({ url, method });
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') {
        listCalls += 1;
        return response(200, { data: listCalls === 1 ? [] : [{ id: 'late-visible-id', name: 'uncertain_submit',
          language: 'pt_BR', status: 'PENDING', category: 'UTILITY', parameter_format: 'NAMED',
          components: [{ type: 'BODY', text: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', example: { body_text_named_params: [{ param_name: 'first_name', example: 'ignored' }] } }] }] });
      }
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') throw new Error('fixture timeout after dispatch');
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      const input = { draftId: draft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: 'd8b515eb-4a6c-45b4-af76-c9258b77a76f' };
      await expect(submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, input)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 503, response: {
        code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_UNCERTAIN', phase: 'AMBIGUOUS', outcome: 'PROVIDER_RESULT_UNCERTAIN', receiptPersistence: 'RECORDED',
      } });
      const ambiguous = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: draft.id, actorUserId: ownerId } });
      expect(ambiguous.phase).toBe('AMBIGUOUS');
      const callsAfterDispatch = fetchMock.mock.calls.length;
      await expect(submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { ...input, idempotencyKey: 'ab3b51f2-2a9c-4c4b-a136-99a57e41d49b' }))
        .rejects.toMatchObject({ response: { code: 'TEMPLATE_RECOVERY_REQUIRED', operationId: ambiguous.id } });
      const replay = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, input);
      expect(replay).toMatchObject({ id: ambiguous.id, phase: 'AMBIGUOUS' });
      expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(callsAfterDispatch);
      expect(calls.filter(({ url }) => url.includes('/debug_token'))).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(calls.filter(({ url, method }) => url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET')).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(calls.filter(({ url, method }) => url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST')).toHaveLength(1);
      const correlated = await submissions.reconcile(ownerId, draft.id, ambiguous.id);
      expect(correlated).toMatchObject({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', providerAcceptance: 'UNKNOWN', remoteTemplateId: 'late-visible-id' });
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: ambiguous.id }, select: {
        phase: true, acceptanceProvenance: true, safeCode: true,
      } })).toEqual({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', acceptanceProvenance: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION', safeCode: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION' });
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.findFirst({ where: { operationId: ambiguous.id }, orderBy: { sequence: 'desc' }, select: {
        phase: true, acceptanceProvenance: true, safeCode: true,
      } })).toEqual({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', acceptanceProvenance: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION', safeCode: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION' });
      expect(calls.filter(({ url }) => url.includes('/debug_token'))).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(calls.filter(({ url, method }) => url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET')).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(calls.filter(({ url, method }) => url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST')).toHaveLength(1);
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('lets an authorized BU admin read and reconcile another actor operation without rebinding its actor', async () => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const mantenedoraId = (await prisma.appUser.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: ownerId }, select: { mantenedoraId: true } })).mantenedoraId;
    const sellerId = await criarUsuario(prisma, mantenedoraId, buId, `template-admin-recovery-${Date.now()}@example.test`, 'ATENDENTE');
    const definition = { name: 'seller_recovery', language: 'pt_BR' as const, category: 'UTILITY' as const,
      body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const draft = await drafts.createDraft(sellerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
      clientRequestId: 'seller-recovery', definition });
    let listCalls = 0;
    let postCalls = 0;
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input);
      const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.includes('/debug_token')) return response(200, { data: { app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true,
        scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'], granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }] } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') {
        listCalls += 1;
        return response(200, { data: listCalls === 1 ? [] : [{ id: 'seller-recovered-id', name: definition.name,
          language: definition.language, status: 'PENDING', category: definition.category, parameter_format: 'NAMED',
          components: [{ type: 'BODY', text: definition.body, example: { body_text_named_params: [{ param_name: 'first_name', example: 'different-example' }] } }] }] });
      }
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') { postCalls += 1; throw new Error('fixture timeout after dispatch'); }
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      await expect(submissions.submitDraft(sellerId, { draftId: draft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: '8e774c25-7cdf-49bf-9f03-24550f05f8a4' }))
        .rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 503, response: { code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_UNCERTAIN' } });
      const operation = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: draft.id } });
      expect((await submissions.getOperation(ownerId, draft.id, operation.id)).phase).toBe('AMBIGUOUS');
      const recovered = await submissions.reconcile(ownerId, draft.id, operation.id);
      expect(recovered).toMatchObject({ id: operation.id, phase: 'ACCEPTED', providerAcceptance: 'UNKNOWN', remoteTemplateId: 'seller-recovered-id' });
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: operation.id }, select: { actorUserId: true, acceptanceProvenance: true } }))
        .toEqual({ actorUserId: sellerId, acceptanceProvenance: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION' });
      expect(postCalls).toBe(1);
      expect(listCalls).toBe(2);
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('confirms a committed PREPARED reservation after an after-commit error without any provider call', async () => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const boundary = app.get<TemplateTransactionBoundary>(TEMPLATE_TRANSACTION_BOUNDARY);
    const definition = { name: 'commit_readback', language: 'pt_BR' as const, category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const draft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: 'commit-readback', definition });
    const fetchMock = vi.fn();
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    const originalRun = boundary.run.bind(boundary);
    const spy = vi.spyOn(boundary, 'run').mockImplementation(async (phase, work) => {
      const result = await originalRun(phase, work);
      if (phase === 'reserve') throw new Error('injected after real reserve commit');
      return result;
    });
    try {
      const input = { draftId: draft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: '64a154fb-7b3a-4cdb-a170-3fef0d67d6cd' };
      const prepared = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, input);
      expect(prepared).toMatchObject({ phase: 'PREPARED', receiptPersistence: 'RECORDED' });
      expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.count({ where: { operationId: prepared.id } })).toBe(1);
      expect((await prisma.messageTemplateDraft.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: draft.id } })).state).toBe('LOCKED');
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.count({ where: { operationId: prepared.id } })).toBe(1);
    } finally { spy.mockRestore(); vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('proves reservation rollback and refuses dispatch when committed-state readback is unavailable', async () => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const boundary = app.get<TemplateTransactionBoundary>(TEMPLATE_TRANSACTION_BOUNDARY);
    const originalRun = boundary.run.bind(boundary);
    let fault: 'rollback' | 'after-commit' | null = null;
    vi.spyOn(boundary, 'run').mockImplementation(async (phase, work) => {
      if (phase !== 'reserve') return originalRun(phase, work);
      if (fault === 'rollback') {
        fault = null;
        return originalRun(phase, async (tx) => {
          await work(tx);
          throw new Error('injected inside reservation transaction before commit');
        });
      }
      const result = await originalRun(phase, work);
      if (fault === 'after-commit') { fault = null; throw new Error('injected after reservation commit'); }
      return result;
    });
    const fetchMock = vi.fn();
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    const definition = (name: string) => ({ name, language: 'pt_BR' as const, category: 'UTILITY' as const,
      body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } });
    try {
      const rolledBackDraft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        clientRequestId: 'reservation-rollback-proof', definition: definition('reservation_rollback_proof') });
      fault = 'rollback';
      await expect(submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: rolledBackDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1,
        expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: rolledBackDraft.definitionFingerprint,
        idempotencyKey: '2d2bb181-4d9a-475f-ae6f-6fffd1138c17' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 503, response: {
        code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED',
      } });
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.count({ where: { draftId: rolledBackDraft.id } })).toBe(0);
      expect((await prisma.messageTemplateDraft.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: rolledBackDraft.id } })).state).toBe('DRAFT');
      expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

      const unknownDraft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        clientRequestId: 'reservation-readback-unavailable', definition: definition('reservation_readback_unknown') });
      fault = 'after-commit';
      await expect(withReadbackFailure(submissions, 'messageTemplateOperation', 'findFirst', () => submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: unknownDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1,
        expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: unknownDraft.definitionFingerprint,
        idempotencyKey: 'db2c2751-5ef8-4847-bad4-34b8de16f20d' }))).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 503, response: {
        code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED',
      } });
      const committed = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: unknownDraft.id } });
      expect(committed.phase).toBe('PREPARED');
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.count({ where: { operationId: committed.id } })).toBe(1);
      expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    } finally { vi.restoreAllMocks(); vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('keeps committed-state recovery readback within three indexed PostgreSQL queries', async () => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const boundary = app.get<TemplateTransactionBoundary>(TEMPLATE_TRANSACTION_BOUNDARY);
    const originalRun = boundary.run.bind(boundary);
    let failAfterReserveCommit = true;
    vi.spyOn(boundary, 'run').mockImplementation(async (phase, work) => {
      const result = await originalRun(phase, work);
      if (phase === 'reserve' && failAfterReserveCommit) {
        failAfterReserveCommit = false;
        throw new Error('injected after real reservation commit');
      }
      return result;
    });
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async () => { throw new Error('provider network is forbidden in readback proof'); });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    const definition = { name: 'bounded_readback', language: 'pt_BR' as const, category: 'UTILITY' as const,
      body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    try {
      const draft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        clientRequestId: 'bounded-readback', definition });
      const prepared = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: draft.id, expectedRevision: 1,
        expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint,
        idempotencyKey: '2d2bb181-4d9a-475f-ae6f-6fffd1138c17' });
      expect(prepared).toMatchObject({ phase: 'PREPARED', receiptPersistence: 'RECORDED' });
      const queryEvents: string[] = [];
      prisma.$on('query', (event) => queryEvents.push(event.query));
      const readbackStart = queryEvents.length;
      const readback = await (submissions as unknown as { readback: (operationId: string, actorId: string,
        businessUnitId: string, tenantId: string) => Promise<{ operation: { id: string; phase: string; receipts: Array<{ phase: string }> };
          reservation: { operationId: string } | null; draft: { id: string; state: string } | null }> })
        .readback(prepared.id, ownerId, buId, draft.mantenedoraId);
      const statements = queryEvents.slice(readbackStart);
      expect(readback).toMatchObject({ operation: { id: prepared.id, phase: 'PREPARED', receipts: [{ phase: 'PREPARED' }] },
        reservation: { operationId: prepared.id }, draft: { id: draft.id, state: 'LOCKED' } });
      expect(statements).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(statements[0]).toMatch(/message_template_operations/i);
      expect(statements[1]).toMatch(/message_template_drafts/i);
      expect(statements[1]).toMatch(/message_template_name_reservations/i);
      expect(statements[1]).toMatch(/message_template_submission_receipts/i);
      expect(statements[1]).toMatch(/LATERAL/i);
      expect(fetchMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    } finally { vi.restoreAllMocks(); vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('readbacks claim, ACK, and proven-refusal releases after real PostgreSQL commit errors', async () => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const boundary = app.get<TemplateTransactionBoundary>(TEMPLATE_TRANSACTION_BOUNDARY);
    const originalRun = boundary.run.bind(boundary);
    let failAfter: 'claim' | 'record-outcome' | 'release' | null = null;
    let claimDraftId: string | null = null;
    let postCommitClaimReadback: unknown;
    vi.spyOn(boundary, 'run').mockImplementation(async (phase, work) => {
      const result = await originalRun(phase, work);
      if (phase === failAfter) {
        failAfter = null;
        if (phase === 'claim' && claimDraftId) postCommitClaimReadback = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirst({
          where: { draftId: claimDraftId }, include: { receipts: { orderBy: { sequence: 'desc' }, take: 1 }, reservation: true,
            draft: { select: { state: true, revision: true } } },
        });
        throw new Error(`injected after ${phase} commit`);
      }
      return result;
    });
    let posts = 0;
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input); const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: [] });
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') {
        posts += 1;
        const submittedName = typeof init.body === 'string' ? JSON.parse(init.body).name : '';
        return submittedName === 'fault_release'
          ? response(400, { error: { type: 'OAuthException', message: 'private refusal', code: 100 } })
          : response(200, { id: `fault-${posts}` });
      }
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    const definition = (name: string) => ({ name, language: 'pt_BR' as const, category: 'UTILITY' as const,
      body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } });
    try {
      const claimDraft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        clientRequestId: 'fault-claim-after-commit', definition: definition('fault_claim') });
      claimDraftId = claimDraft.id;
      failAfter = 'claim';
      let claim: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof submissions.submitDraft>> | undefined;
      let claimFailure: unknown;
      try { claim = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: claimDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1,
        expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: claimDraft.definitionFingerprint,
        idempotencyKey: '482d9c1c-29d4-4e36-aa66-4a311ffbd2e9' }); } catch (error) { claimFailure = error; }
      expect(claimFailure, JSON.stringify(postCommitClaimReadback)).toBeUndefined();
      expect(claim).toMatchObject({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', providerAcceptance: 'CONFIRMED' });

      const ackDraft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        clientRequestId: 'fault-ack-after-commit', definition: definition('fault_ack') });
      failAfter = 'record-outcome';
      const ack = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: ackDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1,
        expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: ackDraft.definitionFingerprint,
        idempotencyKey: 'ba7600e8-55fc-4c11-a90e-03c8b4df6539' });
      expect(ack).toMatchObject({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', providerAcceptance: 'CONFIRMED' });

      const releaseDraft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        clientRequestId: 'fault-release-after-commit', definition: definition('fault_release') });
      failAfter = 'release';
      const refused = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: releaseDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1,
        expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: releaseDraft.definitionFingerprint,
        idempotencyKey: 'd37da041-8cac-41a9-91f5-e482b1427086' });
      expect(refused).toMatchObject({ phase: 'REJECTED', safeCode: 'PROVIDER_REFUSED' });
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.count({ where: { operationId: refused.id } })).toBe(0);
      expect(posts).toBe(3);
      expect(fetchMock.mock.calls.filter(([, init]) => init?.method === 'POST')).toHaveLength(3);
    } finally { vi.restoreAllMocks(); vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('keeps known ACK and refusal local-commit uncertainty recoverable without false release or redispatch', async () => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const boundary = app.get<TemplateTransactionBoundary>(TEMPLATE_TRANSACTION_BOUNDARY);
    const originalRun = boundary.run.bind(boundary);
    let failInside: 'claim' | 'record-outcome' | 'release' | null = null;
    vi.spyOn(boundary, 'run').mockImplementation(async (phase, work) => {
      return originalRun(phase, async (tx) => {
        const result = await work(tx);
        if (phase === failInside) { failInside = null; throw new Error(`injected inside ${phase} transaction before commit`); }
        return result;
      });
    });
    let postCount = 0;
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input); const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: [] });
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') {
        postCount += 1;
        return postCount === 1 ? response(200, { id: 'known-ack-local-uncertain' })
          : response(400, { error: { type: 'OAuthException', message: 'private refusal', code: 100 } });
      }
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    const definition = (name: string) => ({ name, language: 'pt_BR' as const, category: 'UTILITY' as const,
      body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } });
    try {
      const ackDraft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        clientRequestId: 'known-ack-local-uncertain', definition: definition('known_ack_uncertain') });
      const ackInput = { draftId: ackDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: ackDraft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: 'f074e623-0ebc-4538-80b8-55a809ae7fa1' };
      failInside = 'record-outcome';
      await expect(submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, ackInput)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 503, response: {
        code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', operationId: expect.any(String), outcome: 'ACCEPTED', phase: 'ACCEPTED',
        providerAcceptance: 'CONFIRMED', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED',
        message: 'A Meta aceitou o cadastro, mas a conclusão local não foi confirmada; consulte esta operação antes de continuar',
      } });
      const ackOperation = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: ackDraft.id } });
      expect(ackOperation.phase).toBe('SENDING');
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.count({ where: { operationId: ackOperation.id } })).toBe(1);
      const replay = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, ackInput);
      expect(replay).toMatchObject({ id: ackOperation.id, phase: 'SENDING', replayed: true });

      const refusedDraft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        clientRequestId: 'refusal-release-local-uncertain', definition: definition('refusal_release_uncertain') });
      failInside = 'release';
      await expect(submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: refusedDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1,
        expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: refusedDraft.definitionFingerprint,
        idempotencyKey: 'a6f6dbb1-c47d-4524-b7e3-24b68b63731d' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 503, response: {
        code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED',
      } });
      const refusedOperation = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: refusedDraft.id } });
      expect(refusedOperation.phase).toBe('SENDING');
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.count({ where: { operationId: refusedOperation.id } })).toBe(1);

      const claimDraft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        clientRequestId: 'claim-rollback-before-commit', definition: definition('claim_rollback_before_commit') });
      failInside = 'claim';
      await expect(submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: claimDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1,
        expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: claimDraft.definitionFingerprint,
        idempotencyKey: 'a4bf71d2-46cc-40fc-9539-659b94f7a7e6' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 503, response: {
        code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED',
      } });
      const rolledBackClaim = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: claimDraft.id } });
      expect(rolledBackClaim.phase).toBe('PREPARED');
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.count({ where: { operationId: rolledBackClaim.id } })).toBe(1);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.count({ where: { operationId: rolledBackClaim.id } })).toBe(1);
      expect(postCount).toBe(2);
    } finally { vi.restoreAllMocks(); vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('fails closed when post-commit readback is unavailable at claim, ACK, or refusal release', async () => {

      return result;
    });
    let posts = 0;
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {

        posts += 1;
        const name = typeof init.body === 'string' ? JSON.parse(init.body).name : '';
        return name === 'readback_release' ? response(400, { error: { type: 'OAuthException', message: 'private refusal', code: 100 } })

      failAfter = 'claim';

        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: claimDraft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: '47aaf93b-40ea-4f8a-b868-fefbc9ab9794' })))

        clientRequestId: 'ack-postcommit-readback-recorded', definition: definition('readback_ack_recorded') });
      failAfter = 'record-outcome';
