        expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: ackReadbackDraft.definitionFingerprint,
        idempotencyKey: '788a2755-0f6d-4759-a022-03c24e3342e7' });
      expect(recoveredAck).toMatchObject({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', providerAcceptance: 'CONFIRMED',
        acceptanceProvenance: 'ACKNOWLEDGED', receiptPersistence: 'RECORDED' });

      const ackDraft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        clientRequestId: 'ack-readback-unavailable', definition: definition('readback_ack') });
      failAfter = 'record-outcome';
      await expect(withReadbackFailure(submissions, 'messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt', 'findFirst', () => submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: ackDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: ackDraft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: '24b811a2-fcc3-4ab1-98af-2e7072611ed9' })))
        .rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 503, response: { code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', phase: 'ACCEPTED',
          outcome: 'ACCEPTED', providerAcceptance: 'CONFIRMED', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED' } });
      expect((await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: ackDraft.id } })).phase).toBe('ACCEPTED');

      const refusedDraft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        clientRequestId: 'release-readback-unavailable', definition: definition('readback_release') });
      failAfter = 'release';
      await expect(withReadbackFailure(submissions, 'messageTemplateOperation', 'findFirst', () => submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: refusedDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: refusedDraft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: 'da111e4e-e3c8-4338-99be-1211340b0b87' })))
        .rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 503, response: { code: 'TEMPLATE_SUBMISSION_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', phase: 'REJECTED',
          safeCode: 'PROVIDER_REFUSED', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED' } });
      expect((await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: refusedDraft.id } })).phase).toBe('REJECTED');
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.count({ where: { operation: { draftId: refusedDraft.id } } })).toBe(0);
      expect(posts).toBe(3);
    } finally { vi.restoreAllMocks(); vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('measures five waves of twenty independent quota-scoped template intents', async () => {
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const intents: Array<{ actorId: string; buId: string; draftId: string; fingerprint: string; key: string; name: string }> = [];
    const targets: string[] = [];
    for (let index = 0; index < 100; index += 1) {
      const tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, `Template PCV ${index}`, `template-pcv-${Date.now()}-${index}@example.test`);
      const createdChannel = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, tenant.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', `template-pcv-phone-${Date.now()}-${index}`);
      const target = `waba-pcv-${index}`;
      targets.push(target);
      await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: createdChannel.channel.id }, data: {
        wabaId: target, status: 'DESCONECTADO', receiptVerifiedAt: null,
      } });
      const definition = { name: `pcv_template_${String(index).padStart(3, '0')}`, language: 'pt_BR' as const,
        category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
      const draft = await drafts.createDraft(tenant.owner.id, { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id,
        channelId: createdChannel.channel.id, expectedGeneration: 1,
        clientRequestId: `template-pcv-${index}`, definition });
      intents.push({ actorId: tenant.owner.id, buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, draftId: draft.id,
        fingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint, key: `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(index + 1).padStart(12, '0')}`,
        name: definition.name });
    }

    let grantCalls = 0;
    let preflightCalls = 0;
    let postCalls = 0;
    const postPayloads: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
    const accountReferences = new Set<string>();
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input); const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') {
        grantCalls += 1;
        return response(200, { data: { app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true,
          scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
          granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: targets }] } });
      }
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') { preflightCalls += 1; return response(200, { data: [] }); }
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') {
        postCalls += 1;
        const payload = JSON.parse(String(init.body)) as Record<string, unknown>;
        postPayloads.push(payload);
        return response(200, { id: `template-pcv-remote-${String(payload.name)}` });
      }
      throw new Error(`unexpected PCV request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    const waveDurationsMs: number[] = [];
    try {
      for (let wave = 0; wave < 5; wave += 1) {
        const waveStartedAt = performance.now();
        const waveIntents = intents.slice(wave * 20, (wave + 1) * 20);
        const waveResults = await Promise.allSettled(waveIntents.map((intent) =>
          submissions.submitDraft(intent.actorId, { draftId: intent.draftId, expectedRevision: 1,
            expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: intent.fingerprint, idempotencyKey: intent.key })));
        expect(waveResults.every((result) => result.status === 'fulfilled')).toBe(true);
        const outcomes = waveResults.map((result) => {
          if (result.status === 'rejected') throw result.reason;
          return result.value;
        });
        expect(outcomes).toHaveLength(20);
        expect(outcomes.every((outcome) => outcome.phase === 'ACCEPTED')).toBe(true);
        waveDurationsMs.push(Number((performance.now() - waveStartedAt).toFixed(2)));
        const persisted = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findMany({ where: { draftId: { in: waveIntents.map((item) => item.draftId) } },
          select: { id: true, draftId: true, buId: true, actorUserId: true, accountReference: true, phase: true, remoteTemplateId: true } });
        expect(persisted).toHaveLength(20);
        expect(new Set(persisted.map((row) => row.accountReference)).size).toBe(20);
        persisted.forEach((row) => accountReferences.add(row.accountReference));
        const operationIds = outcomes.map((outcome) => outcome.id);
        const receipts = await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.findMany({ where: { operationId: { in: operationIds } },
          orderBy: [{ operationId: 'asc' }, { sequence: 'asc' }],
          select: { operationId: true, phase: true } });
        const reservations = await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.findMany({ where: { operationId: { in: operationIds } },
          select: { operationId: true } });
        for (let offset = 0; offset < waveIntents.length; offset += 1) {
          const intent = waveIntents[offset];
          const operation = persisted.find((row) => row.id === outcomes[offset].id);
          expect(operation).toMatchObject({ draftId: intent.draftId, buId: intent.buId,
            actorUserId: intent.actorId, phase: 'ACCEPTED', remoteTemplateId: `template-pcv-remote-${intent.name}` });
          expect(receipts.filter((row) => row.operationId === outcomes[offset].id).map((row) => row.phase))
            .toEqual(['PREPARED', 'SENDING', 'ACCEPTED']);
          expect(reservations.filter((row) => row.operationId === outcomes[offset].id)).toHaveLength(1);
        }
      }
      const elapsedMs = Number(waveDurationsMs.reduce((sum, duration) => sum + duration, 0).toFixed(2));
      const indexPlan = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ 'QUERY PLAN': unknown }>>`
        EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS, FORMAT JSON)
        SELECT id FROM message_template_operations
        WHERE bu_id = ${intents[0].buId} AND actor_user_id = ${intents[0].actorId}
          AND idempotency_key = ${intents[0].key}::uuid
      `;
      const uniqueIndex = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ indexdef: string }>>`
        SELECT indexdef FROM pg_indexes WHERE schemaname = current_schema()
          AND indexname = 'mt_ops_bu_actor_key_uq'
      `;
      const storage = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ relationBytes: bigint; totalBytes: bigint }>>`
        SELECT pg_relation_size('message_template_operations') AS "relationBytes",
          pg_total_relation_size('message_template_operations') AS "totalBytes"
      `;
      const evidence = { evidenceType: 'disposable-postgresql-template-intent-contention', waves: 5,
        intentsPerWave: 20, intentCount: intents.length, distinctActors: new Set(intents.map((item) => item.actorId)).size,
        distinctBusinessUnits: new Set(intents.map((item) => item.buId)).size,
        distinctAccounts: accountReferences.size,
        quotaPolicy: 'one actor and one account per intent; no quota bypass',
        providerCalls: { grants: grantCalls, preflightGets: preflightCalls, createPosts: postCalls },
        indexedLookup: indexPlan[0]?.['QUERY PLAN'], operationStorageBytes: {
          relation: Number(storage[0]?.relationBytes ?? 0), total: Number(storage[0]?.totalBytes ?? 0),
        }, writeCounts: { operations: await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.count(),
          receipts: await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.count(), reservations: await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.count() },
        waveDurationsMs, elapsedMs, intentsPerSecond: Number((intents.length / (elapsedMs / 1000)).toFixed(2)),
        limit: 'Bounded disposable fixture measurement; not a production capacity or latency claim.' };
      console.log(`[MESSAGE-TEMPLATE-PCV]${JSON.stringify(evidence)}`);
      expect(evidence).toMatchObject({ intentCount: 100, distinctActors: 100, distinctBusinessUnits: 100,
        distinctAccounts: 100, providerCalls: { grants: 100, preflightGets: 100, createPosts: 100 } });
      const payloadByName = new Map(postPayloads.map((payload) => [payload.name, payload]));
      expect(payloadByName.size).toBe(100);
      for (const intent of intents) {
        expect(payloadByName.get(intent.name)).toEqual({ name: intent.name, language: 'pt_BR', category: 'UTILITY',
          parameter_format: 'NAMED', components: [{ type: 'BODY', text: 'Olá {{first_name}}.',
            example: { body_text_named_params: [{ param_name: 'first_name', example: 'Ana' }] } }] });
      }
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.count()).toBe(100);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.count()).toBe(300);
      const lookupDocument = indexPlan[0]?.['QUERY PLAN'] as Array<{ Plan?: Record<string, unknown> }> | undefined;
      const lookupNode = lookupDocument?.[0]?.Plan;
      expect(lookupNode?.['Node Type']).toMatch(/Index Scan/);
      expect(Number(lookupNode?.['Actual Rows'] ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
      expect(lookupNode?.['Actual Loops']).toBe(1);
      expect(lookupNode?.['Rows Removed by Filter'] ?? 0).toBe(0);
      expect(Number(lookupNode?.['Shared Hit Blocks'] ?? 0) + Number(lookupNode?.['Shared Read Blocks'] ?? 0)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(8);
      expect(uniqueIndex[0]?.indexdef).toMatch(/UNIQUE INDEX.*\(bu_id, actor_user_id, idempotency_key\)/i);
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('measures scoped draft and observation reads against high-cardinality PostgreSQL fixtures', async () => {
    const draftsService = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const context = await (app.get(TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_RESOLVER) as TemplateManagementResolver)
      .resolve({ buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1 });
    const hash = (value: string) => createHash('sha256').update(value).digest('hex');
    const contextIdentity = { mantenedoraId: context.context.mantenedoraId, buId: context.context.buId,
      channelId: context.context.channelId, accountReference: context.context.accountReference,
      generation: context.context.generation, pairingEpoch: context.context.pairingEpoch,
      providerResourceIdentityId: context.context.providerResourceIdentityId,
      activeCredentialVersion: context.context.activeCredentialVersion };
    const contextKey = hash(JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(contextIdentity).sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b)))));
    const cursorHash = hash(JSON.stringify('first-page'));
    const now = new Date();
    const ids = Array.from({ length: 1000 }, (_, index) => `e4000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(index + 1).padStart(12, '0')}`);
    const names = ids.map((_, index) => `eps_template_${String(index).padStart(4, '0')}`);
    const definition = (name: string) => ({ name, language: 'pt_BR', category: 'UTILITY', body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } });
    const draftRows = ids.map((id, index) => ({ id, mantenedoraId: context.context.mantenedoraId, buId,
      creatorUserId: ownerId, channelId: channel1, providerResourceIdentityId: context.context.providerResourceIdentityId,
      accountReference: context.context.accountReference, channelGeneration: context.context.generation,
      pairingEpoch: context.context.pairingEpoch, activeCredentialVersion: context.context.activeCredentialVersion,
      clientRequestId: `eps-${index}`, requestHash: hash(`draft-${index}`), revision: 1,
      definition: definition(names[index]) }));
    await prisma.messageTemplateDraft.createMany({ data: draftRows });
    await prisma.messageTemplateRevision.createMany({ data: draftRows.map((row) => ({ draftId: row.id,
      revision: 1, definition: row.definition })) });
    const operationIds = ids.map((_, index) => `e5000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(index + 1).padStart(12, '0')}`);
    await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.createMany({ data: ids.map((draftId, index) => ({
      id: operationIds[index], mantenedoraId: context.context.mantenedoraId, buId, draftId, actorUserId: ownerId,
      idempotencyKey: `e6000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(index + 1).padStart(12, '0')}`,
      requestHash: hash(`request-${index}`), expectedFingerprint: hash(`fingerprint-${index}`), revision: 1,
      definitionSnapshot: draftRows[index].definition, channelId: channel1,
      providerResourceIdentityId: context.context.providerResourceIdentityId,
      accountReference: context.context.accountReference, channelGeneration: context.context.generation,
      pairingEpoch: context.context.pairingEpoch, activeCredentialVersion: context.context.activeCredentialVersion,
      phase: 'ACCEPTED' as const, dispatchClaimId: `e9000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(index + 1).padStart(12, '0')}`,
      remoteTemplateId: `eps_remote_${index}`, acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId: `eps_remote_${index}`,
      acceptanceProvenance: 'ACKNOWLEDGED' as const,
    })) });
    const detailOperationIds = Array.from({ length: 14 }, (_, index) => `e7000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(index + 1).padStart(12, '0')}`);
    await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.createMany({ data: detailOperationIds.map((id, index) => ({
      id, mantenedoraId: context.context.mantenedoraId, buId, draftId: ids[999], actorUserId: ownerId,
      idempotencyKey: `e8000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(index + 1).padStart(12, '0')}`,
      requestHash: hash(`detail-request-${index}`), expectedFingerprint: hash(`detail-fingerprint-${index}`), revision: index + 2,
      definitionSnapshot: draftRows[0].definition, channelId: channel1,
      providerResourceIdentityId: context.context.providerResourceIdentityId, accountReference: context.context.accountReference,
      channelGeneration: context.context.generation, pairingEpoch: context.context.pairingEpoch,
      activeCredentialVersion: context.context.activeCredentialVersion, phase: 'ACCEPTED' as const,
      dispatchClaimId: `e9100000-0000-4000-8000-${String(index + 1).padStart(12, '0')}`,
      remoteTemplateId: `eps_detail_remote_${index}`, acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId: `eps_detail_remote_${index}`,
      acceptanceProvenance: 'ACKNOWLEDGED' as const,
    })) });
    await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.createMany({ data: operationIds.flatMap((operationId, index) => [
      { operationId, sequence: 1, phase: 'PREPARED' as const, safeCode: 'RESERVED' },
      { operationId, sequence: 2, phase: 'SENDING' as const, safeCode: 'DISPATCH_CLAIMED' },
      { operationId, sequence: 3, phase: 'ACCEPTED' as const, remoteTemplateId: `eps_remote_${index}`,
        acceptanceProvenance: 'ACKNOWLEDGED' as const },
    ]) });
    const detailReceiptCount = 500;
    await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.createMany({ data: detailOperationIds
      .flatMap((operationId) => Array.from({ length: detailReceiptCount }, (_, index) => ({ operationId,
        sequence: index + 1, phase: 'ACCEPTED' as const, acceptanceProvenance: 'ACKNOWLEDGED' as const }))) });
    await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.createMany({ data: operationIds.map((operationId, index) => ({
      accountReference: context.context.accountReference, name: names[index], language: 'pt_BR', operationId,
    })) });
    await prisma.messageTemplateObservationLease.create({ data: { contextKey, mantenedoraId: context.context.mantenedoraId,
      buId, channelId: channel1, accountReference: context.context.accountReference,
      channelGeneration: context.context.generation, pairingEpoch: context.context.pairingEpoch,
      providerResourceIdentityId: context.context.providerResourceIdentityId,
      activeCredentialVersion: context.context.activeCredentialVersion ?? 0, fence: 1n } });
    const remoteIds = ids.map((_, index) => `eps_catalog_${String(index).padStart(4, '0')}`);
    await prisma.messageTemplateRemoteObservation.createMany({ data: remoteIds.map((remoteTemplateId, index) => ({
      contextKey, remoteTemplateId, name: names[index], language: 'pt_BR', status: 'APPROVED', category: 'UTILITY',
      parameterFormat: 'NAMED', components: [{ type: 'BODY', text: 'Olá {{first_name}}.' }],
      semanticFingerprint: hash(`semantic-${index}`), supported: true, available: true, observedEligible: true,
      observedAt: now, observedFence: 1n,
    })) });
    await prisma.messageTemplateCatalogPage.create({ data: { contextKey, cursorHash, remoteTemplateIds: remoteIds.slice(0, 50),
      available: true, observedAt: now, observedFence: 1n } });
    await prisma.$executeRaw`ANALYZE message_template_drafts`;
    await prisma.$executeRaw`ANALYZE message_template_operations`;
    await prisma.$executeRaw`ANALYZE message_template_submission_receipts`;
    await prisma.$executeRaw`ANALYZE message_template_remote_observations`;

    const queryEvents: Array<{ query: string; params: string; duration: number }> = [];
    prisma.$on('query', (event) => queryEvents.push(event));
    const pageEventStart = queryEvents.length;
    const page20 = await draftsService.listDrafts(ownerId, buId, { view: 'mine', page: 1, limit: 20 });
    const page20Events = queryEvents.slice(pageEventStart);
    const page50Start = queryEvents.length;
    const page50 = await draftsService.listDrafts(ownerId, buId, { view: 'mine', page: 1, limit: 50 });
    const page50Events = queryEvents.slice(page50Start);
    const deepPageStart = queryEvents.length;
    const deepPage = await draftsService.listDrafts(ownerId, buId, { view: 'mine', page: 26, limit: 20 });
    const deepPageEvents = queryEvents.slice(deepPageStart);
    const lastPageStart = queryEvents.length;
    const lastPage = await draftsService.listDrafts(ownerId, buId, { view: 'mine', page: 50, limit: 20 });
    const lastPageEvents = queryEvents.slice(lastPageStart);
    const beyondPageStart = queryEvents.length;
    const beyondPage = await draftsService.listDrafts(ownerId, buId, { view: 'mine', page: 51, limit: 20 });
    const beyondPageEvents = queryEvents.slice(beyondPageStart);
    const detailStart = queryEvents.length;
    const detail = await draftsService.getDraft(ownerId, buId, ids[999]);
    const detailEvents = queryEvents.slice(detailStart);
    const operationDetailStart = queryEvents.length;
    const operationDetail = await app.get(TemplateSubmissionService).getOperation(ownerId, ids[999], detailOperationIds[13]);
    const operationDetailEvents = queryEvents.slice(operationDetailStart);
    const catalogStart = queryEvents.length;
    const catalog = await app.get(TemplateObservationService).getCatalog(ownerId, buId, channel1, null);
    const catalogEvents = queryEvents.slice(catalogStart);
    const pageEvents = { first20: page20Events, first50: page50Events, deep: deepPageEvents, last: lastPageEvents, beyond: beyondPageEvents };
    const pageQueries = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(pageEvents).map(([label, events]) => [label,
      events.find((event) => event.query.includes('message_template_drafts') && event.query.includes('creator_user_id') && /LIMIT/i.test(event.query))]));
    const draftQuery = pageQueries.first20;
    const detailQuery = detailEvents.find((event) => event.query.includes('message_template_operations')
      && event.query.includes('message_template_submission_receipts') && /LATERAL/i.test(event.query));
    const catalogQuery = catalogEvents.find((event) => event.query.includes('message_template_remote_observations') && event.query.includes('remote_template_id'));
    const explain = async (event: typeof queryEvents[number] | undefined) => event
      ? await prisma.$queryRawUnsafe<Array<{ 'QUERY PLAN': unknown }>>(
        `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS, FORMAT JSON) ${event.query}`, ...JSON.parse(event.params) as unknown[])
      : [];
    const [draftPlan, detailPlan, catalogPlan, ...pagePlans] = await Promise.all([
      explain(draftQuery), explain(detailQuery), explain(catalogQuery),
      ...Object.values(pageQueries).map((event) => explain(event)),
    ]);
    const planLoops = (plan: typeof draftPlan): number | null => {
      const root = (plan[0]?.['QUERY PLAN'] as Array<{ Plan?: Record<string, unknown> }> | undefined)?.[0]?.Plan;
      const nodes: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
      const visit = (node: Record<string, unknown> | undefined): void => {
        if (!node) return;
        nodes.push(node);
        for (const child of (node['Plans'] as Array<Record<string, unknown>> | undefined) ?? []) visit(child);
      };
      visit(root);
      const operation = nodes.find((node) => node['Relation Name'] === 'message_template_operations');
      return operation ? Number(operation['Actual Loops'] ?? 0) : null;
    };
    const pageLimit = (plan: typeof draftPlan): { rows: number; loops: number } | null => {
      const root = (plan[0]?.['QUERY PLAN'] as Array<{ Plan?: Record<string, unknown> }> | undefined)?.[0]?.Plan;
      const nodes: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
      const visit = (node: Record<string, unknown> | undefined): void => {
        if (!node) return;
        nodes.push(node);
        for (const child of (node['Plans'] as Array<Record<string, unknown>> | undefined) ?? []) visit(child);
      };
      visit(root);
      const pageCte = nodes.find((node) => node['Subplan Name'] === 'CTE page_drafts');
      return pageCte?.['Node Type'] === 'Limit'
        ? { rows: Number(pageCte['Actual Rows'] ?? Number.NaN), loops: Number(pageCte['Actual Loops'] ?? Number.NaN) }
        : null;
    };
    const listPageEvidence = Object.entries(pageEvents).map(([label, events], index) => {
      const result = { first20: page20, first50: page50, deep: deepPage, last: lastPage, beyond: beyondPage }[label as 'first20' | 'first50' | 'deep' | 'last' | 'beyond'];
      const plan = pagePlans[index] as typeof draftPlan;
      return { label, returnedRows: result.items.length, total: result.total, pageCount: events.length,
        rowQueryCount: events.filter((event) => /LIMIT/i.test(event.query) && event.query.includes('message_template_drafts')).length,
        parentCountQueries: events.filter((event) => /COUNT\s*\(/i.test(event.query) && event.query.includes('message_template_drafts')).length,
        pageLimit: pageLimit(plan), operationLookupLoops: planLoops(plan), queryPlan: plan[0]?.['QUERY PLAN'] };
    });
    const sizes = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ drafts: bigint; operations: bigint; receipts: bigint; observations: bigint }>>`
      SELECT pg_total_relation_size('message_template_drafts') AS drafts,
        pg_total_relation_size('message_template_operations') AS operations,
        pg_total_relation_size('message_template_submission_receipts') AS receipts,
        pg_total_relation_size('message_template_remote_observations') AS observations
    `;
    const indexSizes = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ indexName: string; bytes: bigint }>>`
      SELECT indexrelname AS "indexName", pg_relation_size(indexrelid) AS bytes
      FROM pg_stat_user_indexes
      WHERE indexrelname IN ('mt_draft_bu_creator_created_idx', 'mt_ops_bu_actor_key_uq',
        'mt_receipt_op_sequence_uq', 'mt_observation_context_remote_uq')
      ORDER BY indexrelname
    `;
    const evidence = { evidenceType: 'disposable-postgresql-template-eps', syntheticOnly: true,
      fixtureRows: { drafts: await prisma.messageTemplateDraft.count(), operations: await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.count(),
        receipts: await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.count(), observations: await prisma.messageTemplateRemoteObservation.count() },
      list: { page20Items: page20.items.length, page50Items: page50.items.length,
        page20QueryCount: page20Events.length, page50QueryCount: page50Events.length,
        pageScenarios: listPageEvidence,
        queryDurationsMs: [...page20Events, ...page50Events].map((event) => Number(event.duration.toFixed(3))),
        relatedQueries: [...page20Events, ...page50Events].filter((event) =>
          event.query.includes('message_template_operations') || event.query.includes('message_template_submission_receipts'))
          .map((event) => event.query),
        draftExplainAnalyzeBuffers: draftPlan[0]?.['QUERY PLAN'] },
      detail: { recentOperations: detail.recentOperations.length,
        receiptCounts: detail.recentOperations.map((operation) => operation.receipts.length),
        operationQueries: detailEvents.filter((event) => event.query.includes('message_template_operations')).map((event) => event.query),
        receiptQueries: detailEvents.filter((event) => event.query.includes('message_template_submission_receipts')).map((event) => event.query),
        queryPlan: detailPlan[0]?.['QUERY PLAN'], directOperationReceiptQueries: operationDetailEvents
          .filter((event) => event.query.includes('message_template_submission_receipts')).map((event) => event.query),
        directOperationReceiptSequences: operationDetail.receipts.map((receipt) => receipt.sequence) },
      catalogue: { items: catalog.items.length, queryCount: catalogEvents.length,
        queryDurationsMs: catalogEvents.map((event) => Number(event.duration.toFixed(3))),
        observationExplainAnalyzeBuffers: catalogPlan[0]?.['QUERY PLAN'] },
      relationBytes: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(sizes[0] ?? {}).map(([key, value]) => [key, Number(value)])),
      indexBytes: indexSizes.map((row) => ({ name: row.indexName, bytes: Number(row.bytes) })),
      fixtureWrites: { drafts: 1000, revisions: 1000, operations: 1014, receipts: 10000,
        reservations: 1000, observationLeases: 1, observations: 1000, cataloguePages: 1 },
      limit: 'Synthetic row cardinality and real PostgreSQL query plans on disposable DB; not a production capacity claim.' };
    console.log(`[MESSAGE-TEMPLATE-EPS]${JSON.stringify(evidence)}`);
    expect(evidence.fixtureRows).toEqual({ drafts: 1000, operations: 1014, receipts: 10000, observations: 1000 });
    expect(evidence.list).toMatchObject({ page20Items: 20, page50Items: 50 });
    expect(evidence.list.pageScenarios.map((scenario) => [scenario.label, scenario.returnedRows, scenario.total])).toEqual([
      ['first20', 20, 1000], ['first50', 50, 1000], ['deep', 20, 1000], ['last', 20, 1000], ['beyond', 0, 1000],
    ]);
    for (const scenario of evidence.list.pageScenarios) {
      expect(scenario.rowQueryCount).toBe(1);
      expect(scenario.parentCountQueries).toBe(1);
      expect(scenario.pageLimit).toEqual({ rows: scenario.returnedRows, loops: 1 });
      expect(scenario.operationLookupLoops).not.toBeNull();
      expect(scenario.operationLookupLoops!).toBeLessThanOrEqual(scenario.returnedRows);
    }
    expect(evidence.list.relatedQueries).toHaveLength(2);
    for (const query of evidence.list.relatedQueries as string[]) {
      expect(query).toMatch(/LEFT JOIN LATERAL/i);
      expect(query).toMatch(/ORDER BY op\.revision DESC LIMIT 1/i);
      expect(query).toMatch(/page_drafts AS MATERIALIZED/i);
      expect(query).toMatch(/ORDER BY d\.created_at DESC, d\.id DESC\s+LIMIT \$\d+ OFFSET \$\d+[\s\S]*LEFT JOIN LATERAL/i);
    }
    expect(evidence.detail.recentOperations).toBe(11);
    expect(evidence.detail.receiptCounts).toHaveLength(11);
    expect(evidence.detail.receiptCounts.every((count) => count === 10)).toBe(true);
    expect(evidence.detail.operationQueries).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(evidence.detail.operationQueries[0]).toMatch(/LEFT JOIN LATERAL/i);
    expect(evidence.detail.operationQueries[0]).toMatch(/ORDER BY revision DESC LIMIT 11/i);
    expect(evidence.detail.operationQueries[0]).toMatch(/ORDER BY sequence DESC LIMIT 10/i);
    expect(evidence.detail.receiptQueries).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(evidence.detail.directOperationReceiptQueries).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(evidence.detail.directOperationReceiptQueries[0]).toMatch(/ORDER BY .*sequence.*DESC.*LIMIT/i);
    expect(evidence.detail.directOperationReceiptSequences).toEqual(Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, index) => 500 - index));
    expect(evidence.catalogue.items).toBe(50);
    const draftPlanRoot = (draftPlan[0]?.['QUERY PLAN'] as Array<{ Plan?: Record<string, unknown> }> | undefined)?.[0]?.Plan;
    const draftNodes: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
    const collectPlanNodes = (node: Record<string, unknown> | undefined): void => {
      if (!node) return;
      draftNodes.push(node);
      for (const child of (node['Plans'] as Array<Record<string, unknown>> | undefined) ?? []) collectPlanNodes(child);
    };
    collectPlanNodes(draftPlanRoot);
    const draftScan = draftNodes.find((node) => node['Relation Name'] === 'message_template_drafts');
    expect(draftScan?.['Node Type']).toMatch(/Index Scan/);
    expect(Number(draftScan?.['Rows Removed by Filter'] ?? 0)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(50);
    expect(Number(draftScan?.['Shared Hit Blocks'] ?? 0) + Number(draftScan?.['Shared Read Blocks'] ?? 0)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(12);
    const operationScan = draftNodes.find((node) => node['Relation Name'] === 'message_template_operations');
    expect(operationScan?.['Index Name']).toBe('mt_ops_draft_revision_uq');
    expect(Number(operationScan?.['Actual Rows'] ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
    expect(Number(operationScan?.['Actual Loops'] ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(50);
    const catalogPlanText = JSON.stringify(catalogPlan[0]?.['QUERY PLAN']);
    expect(catalogPlanText).toContain('mt_observation_context_remote_uq');
    expect(catalogPlanText).not.toContain('Seq Scan');
    const catalogPlanRoot = (catalogPlan[0]?.['QUERY PLAN'] as Array<{ Plan?: Record<string, unknown> }> | undefined)?.[0]?.Plan;
    const catalogNodes: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
    const collectCatalogPlanNodes = (node: Record<string, unknown> | undefined): void => {
      if (!node) return;
      catalogNodes.push(node);
      for (const child of (node['Plans'] as Array<Record<string, unknown>> | undefined) ?? []) collectCatalogPlanNodes(child);
    };
    collectCatalogPlanNodes(catalogPlanRoot);
    const requestedScan = catalogNodes.find((node) => node['Function Name'] === 'unnest');
    const observationScan = catalogNodes.find((node) => node['Relation Name'] === 'message_template_remote_observations');
    expect(requestedScan?.['Node Type']).toBe('Function Scan');
    expect(Number(requestedScan?.['Actual Loops'] ?? 0)).toBe(1);
    expect(Number(requestedScan?.['Actual Rows'] ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)).toBe(50);
    expect(observationScan?.['Index Name']).toBe('mt_observation_context_remote_uq');
    expect(observationScan?.['Node Type']).toMatch(/Index Scan/);
    expect(Number(observationScan?.['Actual Loops'] ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(50);
    expect(Number(observationScan?.['Actual Rows'] ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
    expect(Number(observationScan?.['Rows Removed by Filter'] ?? 0)).toBe(0);
    expect(Number(observationScan?.['Shared Hit Blocks'] ?? 0) + Number(observationScan?.['Shared Read Blocks'] ?? 0)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(150);
    const detailPlanNodes: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
    const collectDetailPlanNodes = (node: Record<string, unknown> | undefined): void => {
      if (!node) return;
      detailPlanNodes.push(node);
      for (const child of (node['Plans'] as Array<Record<string, unknown>> | undefined) ?? []) collectDetailPlanNodes(child);
    };
    collectDetailPlanNodes((evidence.detail.queryPlan as Array<{ Plan?: Record<string, unknown> }> | undefined)?.[0]?.Plan);
    const operationDetailScan = detailPlanNodes.find((node) => node['Relation Name'] === 'message_template_operations');
    const receiptDetailScan = detailPlanNodes.find((node) => node['Relation Name'] === 'message_template_submission_receipts');
    expect(operationDetailScan?.['Index Name']).toBe('mt_ops_draft_revision_uq');
    expect(Number(operationDetailScan?.['Actual Rows'] ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(11);
    expect(Number(operationDetailScan?.['Actual Loops'] ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)).toBe(1);
    expect(receiptDetailScan?.['Node Type']).toMatch(/Index Scan/);
    expect(Number(receiptDetailScan?.['Actual Rows'] ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(10);
    expect(Number(receiptDetailScan?.['Actual Loops'] ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(11);
    expect(Number(receiptDetailScan?.['Rows Removed by Filter'] ?? 0)).toBe(0);
  });

  it.each([
    { label: 'same identifier', candidateId: 'late-ack-authoritative' },
    { label: 'different identifier', candidateId: 'different-get-candidate' },
  ])('lets a known dispatch ACK win over a held recovery GET lease ($label)', async ({ candidateId }) => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const definition = { name: 'late_ack_wins', language: 'pt_BR' as const, category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const draft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: 'late-ack-wins', definition });
    const postStarted = deferred<void>();
    const postHold = deferred<Response>();
    const recoveryListStarted = deferred<void>();
    const recoveryListHold = deferred<Response>();
    let submitPromise: ReturnType<TemplateSubmissionService['submitDraft']> | undefined;
    let reconciliation: ReturnType<TemplateSubmissionService['reconcile']> | undefined;
    let listCalls = 0;
    const calls: Array<{ url: string; method: string }> = [];
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input);
      const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      calls.push({ url, method });
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') {
        listCalls += 1;
        if (listCalls === 1) return response(200, { data: [] });
        recoveryListStarted.resolve();
        return recoveryListHold.promise;
      }
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') {
        postStarted.resolve();
        return postHold.promise;
      }
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      const input = { draftId: draft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: '1d672ddf-0783-46c4-8be4-3d46f6a09672' };
      submitPromise = submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, input);
      await postStarted.promise;
      const operation = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: draft.id, actorUserId: ownerId } });
      await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.update({ where: { id: operation.id }, data: { leaseUntil: new Date(0) } });
      const expired = await submissions.reconcile(ownerId, draft.id, operation.id);
      expect(expired).toMatchObject({ phase: 'AMBIGUOUS' });
      reconciliation = submissions.reconcile(ownerId, draft.id, operation.id);
      await recoveryListStarted.promise;
      const leased = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: operation.id } });
      expect(leased.recoveryOwnerId).toBeTruthy();
      expect(leased.recoveryLeaseUntil?.getTime()).toBeGreaterThan(Date.now());

      postHold.resolve(response(200, { id: 'late-ack-authoritative' }));
      const acknowledged = await submitPromise;
      expect(acknowledged).toMatchObject({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', providerAcceptance: 'CONFIRMED', remoteTemplateId: 'late-ack-authoritative' });
      recoveryListHold.resolve(response(200, { data: [{ id: candidateId, name: 'late_ack_wins',
        language: 'pt_BR', status: 'PENDING', category: 'UTILITY', parameter_format: 'NAMED',
        components: [{ type: 'BODY', text: 'Olá {{first_name}}.' }] }] }));
      await expect(reconciliation).rejects.toMatchObject({ response: { code: 'TEMPLATE_RECONCILIATION_SUPERSEDED' } });
      const final = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: operation.id }, select: {
        phase: true, remoteTemplateId: true, acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId: true, acceptanceProvenance: true, leaseOwnerId: true,
      } });
      expect(final).toEqual({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', remoteTemplateId: 'late-ack-authoritative',
        acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId: 'late-ack-authoritative', acceptanceProvenance: 'ACKNOWLEDGED', leaseOwnerId: operation.leaseOwnerId });
      expect(calls.filter(({ url, method }) => url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST')).toHaveLength(1);
    } finally {
      postHold.resolve(response(200, { id: 'late-ack-authoritative' }));
      recoveryListHold.resolve(response(200, { data: [] }));
      await Promise.allSettled([submitPromise, reconciliation].filter((promise): promise is Promise<unknown> => Boolean(promise)));
      vi.unstubAllGlobals();
    }
  });

  it.each([
    { label: 'same identifier', providerId: 'correlated-template', expectedCode: null, fault: 'none' },
    { label: 'different identifier', providerId: 'provider-template-other', expectedCode: 'PROVIDER_ACK_CONFLICT', fault: 'none' },
    { label: 'rollback keeps correlation without ACK', providerId: 'correlated-template', expectedCode: null, fault: 'rollback' },
    { label: 'different identifier rollback keeps correlation without ACK', providerId: 'provider-template-other', expectedCode: 'PROVIDER_ACK_CONFLICT', fault: 'rollback' },
    { label: 'post-commit throw confirms exact receipt', providerId: 'correlated-template', expectedCode: null, fault: 'postcommit' },
    { label: 'different identifier post-commit throw confirms exact receipt', providerId: 'provider-template-other', expectedCode: 'PROVIDER_ACK_CONFLICT', fault: 'postcommit' },
    { label: 'post-commit readback unavailable stays unconfirmed', providerId: 'correlated-template', expectedCode: null, fault: 'unavailable' },
    { label: 'different identifier readback unavailable stays unconfirmed', providerId: 'provider-template-other', expectedCode: 'PROVIDER_ACK_CONFLICT', fault: 'unavailable' },
  ])('records a provider ACK after a correlated recovery and preserves the association ($label)', async ({ providerId, expectedCode, fault }) => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const definition = { name: `correlated_ack_${providerId.replaceAll('-', '_')}`, language: 'pt_BR' as const,
      category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const draft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
      clientRequestId: `correlated-ack-${providerId}`, definition });
    const postStarted = deferred<void>();
    const postHold = deferred<Response>();
    const listStarted = deferred<void>();
    const listHold = deferred<Response>();
    let submitPromise: ReturnType<TemplateSubmissionService['submitDraft']> | undefined;
    let reconciliation: ReturnType<TemplateSubmissionService['reconcile']> | undefined;
    let faultArmed = false;
    const boundary = app.get<TemplateTransactionBoundary>(TEMPLATE_TRANSACTION_BOUNDARY);
    const originalRun = boundary.run.bind(boundary);
    if (fault !== 'none') vi.spyOn(boundary, 'run').mockImplementation(async (phase, work) => {
      if (!faultArmed || phase !== 'record-outcome') return originalRun(phase, work);
      if (fault === 'rollback') return originalRun(phase, async (tx) => {
        await work(tx);
        throw new Error('injected ACK transaction rollback after writes');
      });
      const result = await originalRun(phase, work);
      if (fault === 'postcommit' || fault === 'unavailable') throw new Error('injected ACK post-commit boundary failure');
      return result;
    });
    let listCalls = 0;
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input);
      const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') {
        listCalls += 1;
        if (listCalls === 1) return response(200, { data: [] });
        listStarted.resolve();
        return listHold.promise;
      }
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') {
        postStarted.resolve();
        return postHold.promise;
      }
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      submitPromise = submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: draft.id, expectedRevision: 1,
        expectedGeneration: 1, expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint,
        idempotencyKey: providerId === 'correlated-template'
          ? 'c1a9dff3-9762-4d5b-8a0e-5df80a2f5b17' : '27dc070c-2445-4cbc-a11f-6085be6f57c9' });
      await postStarted.promise;
      const operation = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: draft.id, actorUserId: ownerId } });
      await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.update({ where: { id: operation.id }, data: { leaseUntil: new Date(0) } });
      expect(await submissions.reconcile(ownerId, draft.id, operation.id)).toMatchObject({ phase: 'AMBIGUOUS' });
      reconciliation = submissions.reconcile(ownerId, draft.id, operation.id);
      await listStarted.promise;
      listHold.resolve(response(200, { data: [{ id: 'correlated-template', name: definition.name,
        language: 'pt_BR', status: 'PENDING', category: 'UTILITY', parameter_format: 'NAMED',
        components: [{ type: 'BODY', text: 'Olá {{first_name}}.' }] }] }));
      await expect(reconciliation).resolves.toMatchObject({ phase: 'ACCEPTED',
        remoteTemplateId: 'correlated-template', providerAcceptance: 'UNKNOWN' });
      faultArmed = true;
      postHold.resolve(response(200, { id: providerId }));
      let acknowledged: Awaited<NonNullable<typeof submitPromise>>;
      if (fault === 'rollback') {
        await expect(submitPromise).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 503, response: {
          providerAcceptance: 'CONFIRMED', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED',
        } });
        acknowledged = await submissions.getOperation(ownerId, draft.id,
          (await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: draft.id } })).id) as Awaited<NonNullable<typeof submitPromise>>;
        expect(acknowledged).toMatchObject({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', providerAcceptance: 'UNKNOWN',
          remoteTemplateId: 'correlated-template', acceptanceProvenance: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION' });
      } else if (fault === 'unavailable') {
        await expect(withReadbackFailure(submissions, 'messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt', 'findFirst',
          () => submitPromise!)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 503, response: {
          providerAcceptance: 'CONFIRMED', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED',
        } });
        acknowledged = await submissions.getOperation(ownerId, draft.id,
          (await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: draft.id } })).id) as Awaited<NonNullable<typeof submitPromise>>;
      } else {
        acknowledged = await submitPromise!;
      }
      if (fault === 'rollback') {
        const rolledBack = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: draft.id } });
        expect(rolledBack).toMatchObject({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', remoteTemplateId: 'correlated-template',
          acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId: null, acceptanceProvenance: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION' });
        expect(await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.count({ where: { operationId: rolledBack.id } })).toBe(4);
        return;
      }
      expect(acknowledged).toMatchObject({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', providerAcceptance: 'CONFIRMED',
        remoteTemplateId: 'correlated-template', safeCode: expectedCode,
        acceptanceProvenance: expectedCode ? 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION' : 'ACKNOWLEDGED' });
      const final = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: operation.id }, select: {
        phase: true, remoteTemplateId: true, acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId: true, acceptanceProvenance: true, safeCode: true,
        leaseOwnerId: true,
      } });
      expect(final).toEqual({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', remoteTemplateId: 'correlated-template',
        acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId: providerId,
        acceptanceProvenance: expectedCode ? 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION' : 'ACKNOWLEDGED', safeCode: expectedCode,
        leaseOwnerId: operation.leaseOwnerId });
      faultArmed = false;
      const receiptCountBeforeReplay = await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.count({ where: { operationId: operation.id } });
      const storedDraft = await prisma.messageTemplateDraft.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: draft.id } });
      const capturedContext = await app.get<TemplateManagementResolver>(TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_RESOLVER)
        .resolve({ buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: operation.channelGeneration });
      const recordAckAgain = Reflect.get(submissions, 'recordAcknowledgedOutcome') as (...args: unknown[]) => Promise<void>;
      await Reflect.apply(recordAckAgain, submissions, [operation.id, operation.leaseOwnerId!, operation.dispatchClaimId!,
        ownerId, storedDraft, operation.requestHash, capturedContext.context, providerId]);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.count({ where: { operationId: operation.id } }))
        .toBe(receiptCountBeforeReplay);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: operation.id } }))
        .toMatchObject({ remoteTemplateId: 'correlated-template', acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId: providerId,
          acceptanceProvenance: expectedCode ? 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION' : 'ACKNOWLEDGED', safeCode: expectedCode });
    } finally {
      postHold.resolve(response(200, { id: providerId }));
      listHold.resolve(response(200, { data: [] }));
      await Promise.allSettled([submitPromise, reconciliation].filter((promise): promise is Promise<unknown> => Boolean(promise)));
      vi.restoreAllMocks();
      vi.unstubAllGlobals();
    }
  });

  it('projects durable ACK authority when its receipt is outside the bounded recent window', async () => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const definition = { name: 'ack_outside_recent_window', language: 'pt_BR' as const,
      category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const draft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
      clientRequestId: 'ack-outside-recent-window', definition });
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input); const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: [] });
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') return response(200, { id: 'durable-ack-remote' });
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      const input = { draftId: draft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: '45da8567-1a84-4816-927f-752f9d848dc9' };
      const accepted = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, input);
      const operationId = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { draftId: draft.id }, select: { id: true } });
      await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.createMany({ data: Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, index) => ({
        operationId: operationId.id, sequence: index + 4, phase: 'ACCEPTED' as const,
        safeCode: `later-${index}`, remoteTemplateId: 'durable-ack-remote', acceptanceProvenance: 'ACKNOWLEDGED' as const,
      })) });
      expect(accepted.receipts.some((receipt) => receipt.acceptanceProvenance === 'ACKNOWLEDGED')).toBe(true);
      const direct = await submissions.getOperation(ownerId, draft.id, operationId.id);
      const detail = await drafts.getDraft(ownerId, buId, draft.id);
      const replay = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, input);
      const reconciled = await submissions.reconcile(ownerId, draft.id, operationId.id);
      for (const projection of [direct, detail.currentOperation, replay, reconciled]) {
        expect(projection).toMatchObject({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', providerAcceptance: 'CONFIRMED',
          acceptanceProvenance: 'ACKNOWLEDGED', remoteTemplateId: 'durable-ack-remote' });
      }
      expect(direct.receipts.some((receipt) => receipt.sequence === 3)).toBe(false);
      expect(detail.currentOperation).not.toHaveProperty('receipts');
      await expect(prisma.messageTemplateOperation.update({ where: { id: operationId.id }, data: {
        acknowledgedRemoteTemplateId: 'replacement-ack',
      } })).rejects.toThrow();
      await expect(prisma.messageTemplateOperation.update({ where: { id: operationId.id }, data: {
        acceptanceProvenance: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION',
      } })).rejects.toThrow();
      await expect(prisma.messageTemplateOperation.update({ where: { id: operationId.id }, data: {
        remoteTemplateId: 'replacement-association',
      } })).rejects.toThrow();
      const ackReceipt = await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.findFirstOrThrow({ where: {
        operationId: operationId.id, sequence: 3,
      }, select: { id: true } });
      await expect(prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.update({ where: { id: ackReceipt.id }, data: {
        safeCode: 'rewritten',
      } })).rejects.toThrow();
      await expect(prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.delete({ where: { id: ackReceipt.id } })).rejects.toThrow();
      const storedOperation = await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: operationId.id } });
      const storedDraft = await prisma.messageTemplateDraft.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: draft.id } });
      const resolved = await app.get<TemplateManagementResolver>(TEMPLATE_MANAGEMENT_RESOLVER).resolve({
        buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: storedOperation.channelGeneration,
      });
      const recordAck = Reflect.get(submissions, 'recordAcknowledgedOutcome') as (...args: unknown[]) => Promise<void>;
      await expect(Reflect.apply(recordAck, submissions, [storedOperation.id, 'forged-owner', 'forged-claim',
        ownerId, storedDraft, storedOperation.requestHash, resolved.context, 'forged-ack']))
        .rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 503, response: { providerAcceptance: 'CONFIRMED', receiptPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED' } });
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: operationId.id } })).toEqual(storedOperation);
      expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it.each([
    { label: 'null ACK association provenance', remoteTemplateId: 'constraint-ack-id', acceptanceProvenance: null, safeCode: null },
    { label: 'null correlated association', remoteTemplateId: null, acceptanceProvenance: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION' as const, safeCode: 'PROVIDER_ACK_CONFLICT' },
    { label: 'null conflict code', remoteTemplateId: 'constraint-associated-id', acceptanceProvenance: 'CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION' as const, safeCode: null },
  ])('rejects an invalid ACK fact with $label', async ({ remoteTemplateId, acceptanceProvenance, safeCode }) => {

  it('releases the global name only after a typed proven refusal and requires a fresh revision/key', async () => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const definition = { name: 'refused_submit', language: 'pt_BR' as const, category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const draft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: 'refused-submit', definition });
    let postCount = 0;
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input);
      const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: [] });
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') {
        postCount += 1;
        if (postCount === 1) return response(400, { error: { type: 'OAuthException', message: 'private provider detail', code: 100 } });
        return response(200, { id: 'after-refusal' });
      }
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      const first = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, {
        draftId: draft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: '1b4c55f7-4af9-4925-9325-b2e30483d902',
      });
      expect(first.phase).toBe('REJECTED');
      expect(first.safeCode).toBe('PROVIDER_REFUSED');
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: first.id }, select: {
        phase: true, safeCode: true, dispatchClaimId: true, acceptanceProvenance: true,
      } })).toMatchObject({ phase: 'REJECTED', safeCode: 'PROVIDER_REFUSED', acceptanceProvenance: null });
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateSubmissionReceipt.findMany({ where: { operationId: first.id }, orderBy: { sequence: 'asc' }, select: {
        phase: true, safeCode: true,
      } })).toEqual([
        { phase: 'PREPARED', safeCode: 'RESERVED' },
        { phase: 'SENDING', safeCode: 'DISPATCH_CLAIMED' },
        { phase: 'REJECTED', safeCode: 'PROVIDER_REFUSED' },
      ]);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.count({ where: { operationId: first.id } })).toBe(0);
      expect((await prisma.messageTemplateDraft.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: draft.id } })).state).toBe('DRAFT');
      await expect(submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, {
        draftId: draft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: draft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: '677f31f4-82ca-4d83-a93d-874f113c5790',
      })).rejects.toMatchObject({ response: { code: 'TEMPLATE_RECOVERY_REQUIRED', operationId: first.id } });
      const saved = await drafts.saveDraft(ownerId, { buId, draftId: draft.id, expectedGeneration: 1, expectedRevision: 1,
        definition: { ...definition, body: 'Atualizado {{first_name}}.' } });
      const second = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, {
        draftId: draft.id, expectedRevision: 2, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: saved.definitionFingerprint!, idempotencyKey: '8b5ca1a9-47c5-405c-9f3c-4c9121c071a1',
      });
      expect(second).toMatchObject({ phase: 'ACCEPTED', remoteTemplateId: 'after-refusal' });
      expect(postCount).toBe(2);
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('reserves account/name/language globally across tenants before a single POST', async () => {
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const otherTenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Template shared namespace', `template-shared-${Date.now()}@example.test`);
    const otherChannel = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, otherTenant.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', `template-shared-phone-${Date.now()}`);
    await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: channel1 }, data: { wabaId: 'waba-shared', status: 'DESCONECTADO', receiptVerifiedAt: null } });
    await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: otherChannel.channel.id }, data: { wabaId: 'waba-shared', status: 'DESCONECTADO', receiptVerifiedAt: null } });
    const definition = { name: 'global_namespace', language: 'pt_BR' as const, category: 'UTILITY' as const, body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } };
    const firstDraft = await app.get(TemplateDraftService).createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: 'global-namespace-a', definition });
    const secondDraft = await app.get(TemplateDraftService).createDraft(otherTenant.owner.id, { buId: otherTenant.businessUnit.id,
      channelId: otherChannel.channel.id, expectedGeneration: 1, clientRequestId: 'global-namespace-b', definition });
    let postCount = 0;
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input);
      const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-shared'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: [] });
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') { postCount += 1; return response(200, { id: `global-${postCount}` }); }
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      const results = await Promise.allSettled([
        submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: firstDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
          expectedDefinitionFingerprint: firstDraft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: '0d94ab3f-1bd4-4505-a9b9-cd73f11f87e1' }),
        submissions.submitDraft(otherTenant.owner.id, { draftId: secondDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
          expectedDefinitionFingerprint: secondDraft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: 'f8875132-26f2-4438-9cb9-38c31f568cac' }),
      ]);
      expect(results.filter((result) => result.status === 'fulfilled')).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(results.filter((result) => result.status === 'rejected')).toHaveLength(1);
      const refusal = results.find((result) => result.status === 'rejected') as PromiseRejectedResult;
      expect(refusal.reason).toMatchObject({ response: { code: 'TEMPLATE_NAME_UNAVAILABLE' } });
      expect(postCount).toBe(1);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateNameReservation.count({ where: { name: definition.name, language: definition.language } })).toBe(1);
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('enforces the 60-per-account sliding-hour quota before a second POST', async () => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const peerId = await criarUsuario(prisma, (await prisma.appUser.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: ownerId }, select: { mantenedoraId: true } })).mantenedoraId,

    const scopeKey = `account:${resolved.context.accountReference}`;
    const now = new Date();
    await prisma.messageTemplateQuotaEvent.createMany({ data: Array.from({ length: 59 }, (_, index) => ({
      kind: 'ACCOUNT', scopeKey, operationId: null,
      occurredAt: index === 58 ? new Date(now.getTime() - 60 * 60_000) : new Date(now.getTime() - index),
    })) });
    const definition = (name: string) => ({ name, language: 'pt_BR' as const, category: 'UTILITY' as const,
      body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } });
    const firstDraft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
      clientRequestId: 'account-quota-first', definition: definition('quota_first') });
    const secondDraft = await drafts.createDraft(peerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
      clientRequestId: 'account-quota-second', definition: definition('quota_second') });
    const thirdId = await criarUsuario(prisma, (await prisma.appUser.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: ownerId }, select: { mantenedoraId: true } })).mantenedoraId,
      buId, `template-quota-third-${Date.now()}@example.test`, 'ATENDENTE');
    const thirdDraft = await drafts.createDraft(thirdId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
      clientRequestId: 'account-quota-third', definition: definition('quota_third') });
    let postCount = 0;
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input);
      const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: [] });
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') { postCount += 1; return response(200, { id: `quota-${postCount}` }); }
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      const first = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: firstDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: firstDraft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: '3fd2dff0-50d8-4829-9a59-a7669600c284' });
      expect(first.phase).toBe('ACCEPTED');
      const second = await submissions.submitDraft(peerId, { draftId: secondDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: secondDraft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: 'bb9b1f9f-2a84-4383-874c-90562e2fd61a' });
      expect(second.phase).toBe('ACCEPTED');
      await expect(submissions.submitDraft(thirdId, { draftId: thirdDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: thirdDraft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: 'fa8936d6-bf84-4e10-a390-574a147fa7d1' }))
        .rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 429, response: { code: 'TEMPLATE_RATE_LIMITED' } });
      expect(postCount).toBe(2);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateQuotaEvent.count({ where: { kind: 'ACCOUNT', scopeKey } })).toBe(60);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.count({ where: { draftId: thirdDraft.id, phase: 'REJECTED' } })).toBe(1);
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
  });

  it('counts the tenth actor attempt and rejects the next one before provider I/O', async () => {
    const drafts = app.get(TemplateDraftService);
    const submissions = app.get(TemplateSubmissionService);
    const actorScope = `actor:${ownerId}`;
    const now = new Date();
    await prisma.messageTemplateQuotaEvent.createMany({ data: Array.from({ length: 9 }, (_, index) => ({
      kind: 'ACTOR', scopeKey: actorScope, operationId: null,
      occurredAt: index === 8 ? new Date(now.getTime() - 60_000) : new Date(now.getTime() - index),
    })) });

      body: 'Olá {{first_name}}.', examples: { first_name: 'Ana' } });

      clientRequestId: 'actor-quota-first', definition: definition('actor_quota_first') });
    const secondDraft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
      clientRequestId: 'actor-quota-second', definition: definition('actor_quota_second') });
    const thirdDraft = await drafts.createDraft(ownerId, { buId, channelId: channel1, expectedGeneration: 1,
      clientRequestId: 'actor-quota-third', definition: definition('actor_quota_third') });
    let postCount = 0;
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}) => {
      const url = String(input);
      const method = init.method ?? 'GET';
      if (url.includes('/debug_token') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: {
        app_id: 'fixture-app', is_valid: true, scopes: ['whatsapp_business_management'],
        granular_scopes: [{ scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['waba-a'] }],
      } });
      if (url.includes('/message_templates?') && method === 'GET') return response(200, { data: [] });
      if (url.endsWith('/message_templates') && method === 'POST') { postCount += 1; return response(200, { id: `actor-quota-${postCount}` }); }
      throw new Error(`unexpected template request ${method} ${url}`);
    });
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock);
    try {
      const first = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: firstDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: firstDraft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: 'b3a2fccd-9496-4096-9130-d8685950e450' });
      expect(first.phase).toBe('ACCEPTED');
      const second = await submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: secondDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: secondDraft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: 'bb8c5c9c-1698-4c0f-a048-7c226fdfd2d5' });
      expect(second.phase).toBe('ACCEPTED');
      const callsAtLimit = fetchMock.mock.calls.length;
      await expect(submissions.submitDraft(ownerId, { draftId: thirdDraft.id, expectedRevision: 1, expectedGeneration: 1,
        expectedDefinitionFingerprint: thirdDraft.definitionFingerprint, idempotencyKey: 'd7a49c0d-c5c4-42c8-9ccf-a5549f098fe9' }))
        .rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 429, response: { code: 'TEMPLATE_RATE_LIMITED' } });
      expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(callsAtLimit);
      expect(postCount).toBe(2);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateQuotaEvent.count({ where: { kind: 'ACTOR', scopeKey: actorScope } })).toBe(10);
      expect(await prisma.messageTemplateOperation.count({ where: { draftId: thirdDraft.id } })).toBe(0);
    } finally { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); }
